=== FILE: scripts\migrate-supabase-to-r2.mjs ===
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Chuyển toàn bộ tệp từ Supabase Storage sang Cloudflare R2.
 *
 *   node scripts/migrate-supabase-to-r2.mjs --dry-run      (chỉ liệt kê, không chép)
 *   node scripts/migrate-supabase-to-r2.mjs                (chép sang R2, KHÔNG xoá bản gốc)
 *   node scripts/migrate-supabase-to-r2.mjs --delete       (chép + xoá bản gốc trên Supabase SAU KHI kiểm tra khớp dung lượng)
 *
 * Chạy lại nhiều lần được: tệp đã có trên R2 (cùng dung lượng) sẽ được bỏ qua.
 * Cần biến môi trường (đặt trong .env.local hoặc PowerShell):
 *   NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY  (khoá "secret"/"service_role" — CHỈ dùng trên máy bạn)
 *   R2_ACCOUNT_ID, R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID, R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY, R2_BUCKET
 */
import { createHash, createHmac } from "node:crypto";
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

// ---- đọc .env.local (nếu có) ----
if (existsSync(".env.local")) {
  for (const line of readFileSync(".env.local", "utf8").split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const m = line.match(/^\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*(.*?)\s*$/);
    if (m && !(m[1] in process.env)) process.env[m[1]] = m[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
  }
}

const args = new Set(process.argv.slice(2));
const DRY = args.has("--dry-run");
const DELETE_AFTER = args.has("--delete");

const need = ["NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL", "SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY", "R2_ACCOUNT_ID", "R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID", "R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY", "R2_BUCKET"];
const missing = need.filter((k) => !process.env[k]);
if (missing.length) {
  console.error("Thiếu biến môi trường:", missing.join(", "));
  process.exit(1);
}

const sb = createClient(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL, process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, {
  auth: { persistSession: false },
});

// ---- ký URL R2 (AWS Signature V4) — cùng thuật toán với src/lib/r2.ts ----
const enc = (s) => encodeURIComponent(s).replace(/[!'()*]/g, (c) => "%" + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase());
const encPath = (p) => p.split("/").map(enc).join("/");
const sha = (s) => createHash("sha256").update(s).digest("hex");
const hmac = (k, d) => createHmac("sha256", k).update(d).digest();

function presign(method, key, headers = {}) {
  const host = `${process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`;
  const path = `/${process.env.R2_BUCKET}/${encPath(key)}`;
  const now = new Date();
  const amz = now.toISOString().replace(/[:-]|\.\d{3}/g, "");
  const date = amz.slice(0, 8);
  const scope = `${date}/auto/s3/aws4_request`;
  const hs = { host, ...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(headers).map(([k, v]) => [k.toLowerCase(), String(v).trim()])) };
  const names = Object.keys(hs).sort();
  const signed = names.join(";");
  const q = {
    "X-Amz-Algorithm": "AWS4-HMAC-SHA256",
    "X-Amz-Credential": `${process.env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID}/${scope}`,
    "X-Amz-Date": amz,
    "X-Amz-Expires": "900",
    "X-Amz-SignedHeaders": signed,
  };
  const cq = Object.keys(q).sort().map((k) => `${enc(k)}=${enc(q[k])}`).join("&");
  const creq = [method, path, cq, names.map((n) => `${n}:${hs[n]}\n`).join(""), signed, "UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD"].join("\n");
  const sts = ["AWS4-HMAC-SHA256", amz, scope, sha(creq)].join("\n");
  const kSign = hmac(hmac(hmac(hmac("AWS4" + process.env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY, date), "auto"), "s3"), "aws4_request");
  const sig = createHmac("sha256", kSign).update(sts).digest("hex");
  return `https://${host}${path}?${cq}&X-Amz-Signature=${sig}`;
}

const TYPES = {
  mp3: "audio/mpeg", m4a: "audio/mp4", wav: "audio/wav", ogg: "audio/ogg", aac: "audio/aac", flac: "audio/flac",
  jpg: "image/jpeg", jpeg: "image/jpeg", png: "image/png", webp: "image/webp", gif: "image/gif", svg: "image/svg+xml",
  pdf: "application/pdf", epub: "application/epub+zip", doc: "application/msword",
  docx: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document", txt: "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
};
const typeOf = (path, fallback) => TYPES[path.split(".").pop().toLowerCase()] || fallback || "application/octet-stream";

// ---- liệt kê tệp trong Supabase ----
async function listAll(bucket, prefix = "") {
  const out = [];
  for (let offset = 0; ; offset += 1000) {
    const { data, error } = await sb.storage.from(bucket).list(prefix, { limit: 1000, offset });
    if (error) throw error;
    for (const it of data ?? []) {
      const full = prefix ? `${prefix}/${it.name}` : it.name;
      if (it.id === null) out.push(...(await listAll(bucket, full)));
      else if (it.name !== ".emptyFolderPlaceholder") out.push({ bucket, path: full, size: it.metadata?.size ?? 0 });
    }
    if (!data || data.length < 1000) break;
  }
  return out;
}

async function r2Size(key) {
  const res = await fetch(presign("HEAD", key), { method: "HEAD" });
  if (res.status === 404) return null;
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HEAD ${key} -> ${res.status}`);
  return Number(res.headers.get("content-length") ?? -1);
}

async function copyOne(f) {
  const key = `${f.bucket}/${f.path}`;
  const existing = await r2Size(key);
  if (existing !== null && existing === f.size && f.size > 0) return "skip";
  if (DRY) return "would-copy";

  const { data: blob, error } = await sb.storage.from(f.bucket).download(f.path);
  if (error) throw new Error(`Tải ${key} từ Supabase lỗi: ${error.message}`);
  const body = Buffer.from(await blob.arrayBuffer());
  const ct = typeOf(f.path, blob.type);
  const res = await fetch(presign("PUT", key, { "content-type": ct }), { method: "PUT", headers: { "Content-Type": ct }, body });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`PUT ${key} -> ${res.status} ${await res.text().catch(() => "")}`);

  const after = await r2Size(key);
  if (after !== body.length) throw new Error(`Kiểm tra dung lượng ${key} không khớp (${after} ≠ ${body.length})`);
  return "copied";
}

// ---- chạy ----
const buckets = ["images", "covers", "books", "music"];
const all = [];
for (const b of buckets) {
  const files = await listAll(b);
  console.log(`• ${b}: ${files.length} tệp, ${(files.reduce((n, f) => n + f.size, 0) / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`);
  all.push(...files);
}
if (DRY) console.log("\n(chế độ --dry-run: chỉ kiểm tra, chưa chép gì)\n");

const stats = { copied: 0, skip: 0, "would-copy": 0, failed: 0 };
const ok = [];
const queue = [...all];
let done = 0;
await Promise.all(
  Array.from({ length: 4 }, async () => {
    for (let f = queue.shift(); f; f = queue.shift()) {
      try {
        const r = await copyOne(f);
        stats[r]++;
        if (r === "copied" || r === "skip") ok.push(f);
      } catch (e) {
        stats.failed++;
        console.error(`  ✗ ${f.bucket}/${f.path}: ${e.message}`);
      }
      if (++done % 10 === 0 || done === all.length) console.log(`  ${done}/${all.length}…`);
    }
  }),
);

console.log("\nKết quả:", stats);

if (DELETE_AFTER && !DRY) {
  if (stats.failed) {
    console.error("Có tệp lỗi nên KHÔNG xoá bản gốc trên Supabase. Hãy chạy lại script cho đến khi hết lỗi.");
    process.exit(1);
  }
  for (const b of buckets) {
    const paths = ok.filter((f) => f.bucket === b).map((f) => f.path);
    for (let i = 0; i < paths.length; i += 100) {
      const { error } = await sb.storage.from(b).remove(paths.slice(i, i + 100));
      if (error) console.error(`  ✗ xoá ${b}:`, error.message);
    }
    if (paths.length) console.log(`  đã xoá ${paths.length} tệp trong Supabase/${b}`);
  }
}
if (stats.failed) process.exit(1);
console.log(DRY ? "Xong (dry-run)." : "Xong. Bước tiếp theo: chạy supabase/after_r2_migration.sql");

=== FILE: src\app\admin\don-dep\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import RequireAuth from "@/components/auth/RequireAuth";
import { STORAGE_FREE_LIMIT, deleteOrphans, formatBytes, scanStorage, type BucketReport } from "@/services/cleanupService";

function Cleanup() {
  const [reports, setReports] = useState<BucketReport[] | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showList, setShowList] = useState(false);

  async function scan() {
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg(null);
    setError(null);
    try {
      setReports(await scanStorage());
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Không quét được");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function clean() {
    if (!reports) return;
    const all = reports.flatMap((r) => r.orphans);
    if (!all.length) return;
    if (!confirm(`Xoá vĩnh viễn ${all.length} tệp không còn dùng (${formatBytes(all.reduce((n, f) => n + f.size, 0))})?`)) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const n = await deleteOrphans(all);
      setMsg(`Đã xoá ${n} tệp.`);
      setReports(await scanStorage());
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Không xoá được");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const totalBytes = reports?.reduce((n, r) => n + r.totalBytes, 0) ?? 0;
  const orphanCount = reports?.reduce((n, r) => n + r.orphans.length, 0) ?? 0;
  const orphanBytes = reports?.reduce((n, r) => n + r.orphanBytes, 0) ?? 0;
  const pct = Math.min(100, (totalBytes / STORAGE_FREE_LIMIT) * 100);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
      <Link href="/admin" className="text-sm text-accent hover:underline">← Quản trị</Link>
      <h1 className="mt-3 font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">Dọn dẹp kho lưu trữ (Cloudflare R2)</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-ink-soft">
        Tìm các tệp không còn được dùng ở đâu (ví dụ ảnh tải lên rồi đóng tab không lưu bài). Tệp mới tải lên trong 24 giờ qua được bỏ qua.
      </p>

      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <button type="button" onClick={scan} disabled={busy} className="btn btn-primary !py-3">{busy ? "Đang xử lý…" : "🔍 Quét kho lưu trữ"}</button>
        {orphanCount > 0 && (
          <button type="button" onClick={clean} disabled={busy} className="btn btn-danger !py-3">
            🗑 Xoá {orphanCount} tệp thừa ({formatBytes(orphanBytes)})
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {msg && <p className="mt-4 text-green-500">{msg}</p>}
      {error && <p className="mt-4 text-red-500">{error}</p>}

      {reports && (
        <>
          <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
              <span className="font-medium text-ink">Tổng dung lượng: {formatBytes(totalBytes)}</span>
              <span className="text-muted">{pct.toFixed(1)}% của 10 GB miễn phí (Cloudflare R2)</span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2 h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-soft">
              <div className={`h-full ${pct > 85 ? "bg-red-600" : "bg-accent"}`} style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface">
            {reports.map((r) => (
              <div key={r.bucket} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-4">
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <p className="font-medium text-ink">{r.label}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted">{r.total} tệp · {formatBytes(r.totalBytes)}</p>
                </div>
                <span className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm ${r.orphans.length ? "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800" : "bg-green-100 text-green-800"}`}>
                  {r.orphans.length ? `${r.orphans.length} thừa · ${formatBytes(r.orphanBytes)}` : "Sạch"}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {orphanCount > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowList(!showList)} className="text-sm text-accent hover:underline">
                {showList ? "Ẩn danh sách" : "Xem danh sách tệp thừa"}
              </button>
              {showList && (
                <ul className="mt-3 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-3 text-xs text-ink-soft">
                  {reports.flatMap((r) => r.orphans).map((f) => (
                    <li key={`${f.bucket}/${f.path}`} className="truncate py-0.5">
                      <span className="text-muted">{f.bucket}/</span>{f.path} — {formatBytes(f.size)}
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function CleanupPage() {
  return <RequireAuth admin><Cleanup /></RequireAuth>;
}

=== FILE: src\app\admin\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import RequireAuth from "@/components/auth/RequireAuth";
import { deletePost, listAllPosts, setPostPublished } from "@/services/postService";
import { deleteSong, listAllSongs, setSongPublished } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Post } from "@/types/post";
import { authorLabel } from "@/types/post";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";
import { formatCount, formatDateTime, wasEdited } from "@/utils/formatDate";

function Times({ created, updated, verb }: { created: string; updated: string; verb: string }) {
  return (
    <>
      {verb}: {formatDateTime(created)}
      {wasEdited(created, updated) && <> · Sửa: {formatDateTime(updated)}</>}
    </>
  );
}

function Admin() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"posts" | "songs">("posts");
  const [onlyPending, setOnlyPending] = useState(true);
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<Post[]>([]);
  const [songs, setSongs] = useState<Song[]>([]);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const [p, s] = await Promise.all([listAllPosts(), listAllSongs()]);
    setPosts(p);
    setSongs(s);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const pendingPosts = posts.filter((p) => !p.is_published).length;
  const pendingSongs = songs.filter((s) => !s.is_published).length;
  const shownPosts = onlyPending ? posts.filter((p) => !p.is_published) : posts;
  const shownSongs = onlyPending ? songs.filter((s) => !s.is_published) : songs;
  const tabCls = (on: boolean) => `btn ${on ? "btn-primary" : "btn-secondary"}`;
  const small = "!py-1.5";

  return (
    <div>
      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Quản trị</p>
      <div className="mb-6 mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">Quản trị nội dung</h1>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Link href="/admin/radio" className="btn btn-secondary">📻 Quản lý Radio</Link>
          <Link href="/admin/don-dep" className="btn btn-secondary">🧹 Dọn dẹp kho lưu trữ</Link>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setTab("posts")} className={tabCls(tab === "posts")}>Bài viết ({pendingPosts} chờ duyệt)</button>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setTab("songs")} className={tabCls(tab === "songs")}>Nhạc ({pendingSongs} chờ duyệt)</button>
        <label className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-ink-soft">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={onlyPending} onChange={(e) => setOnlyPending(e.target.checked)} className="accent-[var(--accent)]" /> Chỉ hiện mục chờ duyệt
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface">
        {tab === "posts" &&
          shownPosts.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/bai-viet/${p.slug}`} className="font-medium text-ink hover:underline">{p.title}</Link>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                  Tác giả: {authorLabel(p)} · đăng bởi {p.author?.full_name ?? "?"} · 👁 {formatCount(p.view_count)}{p.file_name && ` · 📎 ${p.file_name}`}
                </p>
                {p.tags.length > 0 && <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">{p.tags.map((t) => <span key={t} className="chip">#{t}</span>)}</div>}
                <p className="text-xs text-muted"><Times created={p.created_at} updated={p.updated_at} verb="Đăng" /></p>
              </div>
              <button type="button" onClick={async () => { await setPostPublished(p.id, !p.is_published); load(); }}
                className={`btn ${small} ${p.is_published ? "btn-secondary" : "btn-primary"}`}>{p.is_published ? "Ẩn" : "Duyệt"}</button>
              <Link href={`/sua-bai/${p.id}`} className={`btn btn-secondary ${small}`}>✏️ Sửa</Link>
              <button type="button" onClick={async () => { if (confirm("Xoá vĩnh viễn bài này, kèm tệp đính kèm và ảnh trong bài?")) { await deletePost(p); load(); } }}
                className={`btn btn-danger ${small}`}>Xoá</button>
            </div>
          ))}

        {tab === "songs" &&
          shownSongs.map((s) => (
            <div key={s.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/bai-hat/${s.id}`} className="font-medium text-ink hover:underline">{s.title}</Link>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">{s.artist}{s.album_info && ` · ${s.album_info.title}`} · ▶ {formatCount(s.play_count)}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted"><Times created={s.created_at} updated={s.updated_at} verb="Tải lên" /></p>
              </div>
              <button type="button" onClick={async () => { await setSongPublished(s.id, !s.is_published); load(); }}
                className={`btn ${small} ${s.is_published ? "btn-secondary" : "btn-primary"}`}>{s.is_published ? "Ẩn" : "Duyệt"}</button>
              <Link href={`/sua-nhac/${s.id}`} className={`btn btn-secondary ${small}`}>✏️ Sửa</Link>
              <button type="button" onClick={async () => { if (confirm("Xoá vĩnh viễn bài hát, kèm tệp nhạc và ảnh bìa?")) { await deleteSong(s); load(); } }}
                className={`btn btn-danger ${small}`}>Xoá</button>
            </div>
          ))}

        {((tab === "posts" && !shownPosts.length) || (tab === "songs" && !shownSongs.length)) && (
          <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted">Không có mục nào.</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AdminPage() {
  return <RequireAuth admin><Admin /></RequireAuth>;

=== FILE: src\app\admin\radio\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import RequireAuth from "@/components/auth/RequireAuth";
import { usePlayer } from "@/components/music/PlayerProvider";
import { RadioIcon } from "@/components/ui/Icons";
import { listAlbums } from "@/services/albumService";
import { getRadioSettings, listRadioSongs, saveRadioSettings, saveRadioSongs } from "@/services/radioService";
import { listPublishedSongs, songCoverUrl } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";
import { matchSearch } from "@/utils/slug";

function RadioAdmin() {
  const { startRadio } = usePlayer();
  const [songs, setSongs] = useState<Song[]>([]);
  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<Album[]>([]);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"all" | "custom">("all");
  const [shuffle, setShuffle] = useState(true);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [albumPick, setAlbumPick] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([listPublishedSongs(), listAlbums(), getRadioSettings(), listRadioSongs().catch(() => [] as Song[])])
      .then(([s, a, st, chosen]) => {
        setSongs(s);
        setAlbums(a);
        setMode(st.mode);
        setShuffle(st.shuffle);
        setSelected(chosen.map((x) => x.id));
      })
      .catch((e) => setMsg({ ok: false, text: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Không tải được dữ liệu" }))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const byId = useMemo(() => new Map(songs.map((s) => [s.id, s])), [songs]);
  const chosen = selected.map((id) => byId.get(id)).filter((s): s is Song => !!s);
  const available = songs.filter((s) => !selected.includes(s.id) && matchSearch(`${s.title} ${s.artist ?? ""} ${s.album_info?.title ?? ""}`, q));

  const add = (ids: string[]) => setSelected((cur) => [...cur, ...ids.filter((id) => !cur.includes(id))]);
  const remove = (id: string) => setSelected((cur) => cur.filter((x) => x !== id));
  const move = (i: number, d: -1 | 1) =>
    setSelected((cur) => {
      const j = i + d;
      if (j < 0 || j >= cur.length) return cur;
      const next = [...cur];
      [next[i], next[j]] = [next[j], next[i]];
      return next;
    });

  async function save() {
    if (mode === "custom" && !selected.length) {
      return setMsg({ ok: false, text: "Chế độ “danh sách tuỳ chọn” cần ít nhất 1 bài. Hãy thêm bài vào danh sách Radio." });
    }
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg(null);
    try {
      await saveRadioSongs(selected);
      await saveRadioSettings({ mode, shuffle });
      setMsg({ ok: true, text: "Đã lưu cấu hình Radio." });
    } catch (e) {
      setMsg({ ok: false, text: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Không lưu được" });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-muted">Đang tải…</p>;

  const modeCard = (value: "all" | "custom", title: string, desc: string) => (
    <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode(value)} aria-pressed={mode === value}
      className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 text-left transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 ${mode === value ? "border-accent bg-accent-soft" : "border-line bg-surface hover:border-accent/50"}`}>
      <p className="font-semibold text-ink">{mode === value ? "● " : "○ "}{title}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink-soft">{desc}</p>
    </button>
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <Link href="/admin" className="text-sm text-accent hover:underline">← Quản trị</Link>
      <h1 className="mt-3 font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">Quản lý Radio</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-ink-soft">Chọn những bài hát sẽ phát khi người nghe bấm “Bật Radio” trên trang chủ và trang Nhạc.</p>

      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
        {modeCard("all", "Toàn bộ thư viện", "Phát mọi bài hát đã đăng. Bài mới tự động vào Radio.")}
        {modeCard("custom", "Danh sách do quản trị chọn", "Chỉ phát những bài trong danh sách bên dưới, theo thứ tự bạn sắp xếp.")}
      </div>

      <label className="mt-5 flex items-center gap-3 text-ink">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={shuffle} onChange={(e) => setShuffle(e.target.checked)} className="h-5 w-5 accent-[var(--accent)]" />
        <span>Phát ngẫu nhiên <span className="text-sm text-muted">(tắt = phát đúng thứ tự, hết vòng tự lặp lại từ đầu)</span></span>
      </label>

      <div className={`mt-8 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2 ${mode === "all" ? "opacity-50" : ""}`}>
        {/* Cột trái: tất cả bài hát */}
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-4">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-ink">Thư viện nhạc ({available.length})</h2>
          <input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="🔍 Tìm bài hát, ca sĩ, album…" className="field mt-3 !rounded-full" />
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-secondary !py-1.5" onClick={() => add(available.map((s) => s.id))} disabled={!available.length}>＋ Thêm tất cả đang hiện</button>
            <select value={albumPick} onChange={(e) => setAlbumPick(e.target.value)} className="field !w-auto !rounded-full !py-1.5 text-sm">
              <option value="">Thêm cả album…</option>
              {albums.map((a) => <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>{a.title}</option>)}
            </select>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-secondary !py-1.5" disabled={!albumPick}
              onClick={() => { add(songs.filter((s) => s.album_id === albumPick).map((s) => s.id)); setAlbumPick(""); }}>Thêm</button>
          </div>
          <ul className="mt-3 max-h-[28rem] space-y-1 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
            {available.map((s) => (
              <li key={s.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl p-2 hover:bg-soft">
                <Thumb song={s} />
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className="truncate text-sm font-medium text-ink">{s.title}</p>
                  <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">{s.artist ?? "—"}{s.album_info ? ` · ${s.album_info.title}` : ""}</p>
                </div>
                <button type="button" className="btn btn-secondary !px-3 !py-1" onClick={() => add([s.id])} aria-label={`Thêm ${s.title}`}>＋</button>
              </li>
            ))}
            {!available.length && <li className="p-3 text-sm text-muted">Không còn bài nào để thêm.</li>}
          </ul>
        </section>

        {/* Cột phải: danh sách Radio */}
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-ink">Danh sách Radio ({chosen.length})</h2>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger !py-1" disabled={!selected.length} onClick={() => confirm("Bỏ hết bài khỏi danh sách Radio?") && setSelected([])}>Bỏ hết</button>
          </div>
          <ol className="mt-3 max-h-[32rem] space-y-1 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
            {chosen.map((s, i) => (
              <li key={s.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl p-2 hover:bg-soft">
                <span className="w-6 text-center text-xs text-muted">{i + 1}</span>
                <Thumb song={s} />
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className="truncate text-sm font-medium text-ink">{s.title}</p>
                  <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">{s.artist ?? "—"}</p>
                </div>
                <button type="button" className="btn-icon !h-8 !w-8" onClick={() => move(i, -1)} disabled={i === 0} aria-label="Lên">↑</button>
                <button type="button" className="btn-icon !h-8 !w-8" onClick={() => move(i, 1)} disabled={i === chosen.length - 1} aria-label="Xuống">↓</button>
                <button type="button" className="btn-icon !h-8 !w-8 text-[#ef6a6a]" onClick={() => remove(s.id)} aria-label="Bỏ">✕</button>
              </li>
            ))}
            {!chosen.length && <li className="p-3 text-sm text-muted">Chưa có bài nào. Thêm bài từ cột bên trái.</li>}
          </ol>
        </section>
      </div>

      {msg && <p className={`mt-6 ${msg.ok ? "text-green-500" : "text-red-500"}`}>{msg.text}</p>}
      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary !px-7 !py-3" onClick={save} disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Đang lưu…" : "Lưu cấu hình Radio"}</button>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-secondary !py-3" disabled={mode === "custom" ? !chosen.length : !songs.length}
          onClick={() => startRadio(mode === "custom" ? chosen : songs, "Radio Phật Tại Tâm (nghe thử)", shuffle)}>
          <RadioIcon size={18} /> Nghe thử
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Thumb({ song }: { song: Song }) {
  const cover = songCoverUrl(song);
  return cover ? (
    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
    <img src={cover} alt="" className="h-10 w-10 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover" loading="lazy" />
  ) : (
    <span className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-accent-soft">🎵</span>
  );
}

export default function RadioAdminPage() {
  return <RequireAuth admin><RadioAdmin /></RequireAuth>;
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE } from "@/lib/site";
import { serverPublicUrl, serverSupabase } from "@/lib/supabaseServer";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { data: a } = await serverSupabase().from("albums").select("title, description, cover_path").eq("id", id).maybeSingle();
  if (!a) return { title: "Không tìm thấy album" };
  const cover = a.cover_path ? serverPublicUrl("covers", a.cover_path) : null;
  const description = a.description?.slice(0, 180) || `Nghe album “${a.title}” trên Phật Tại Tâm.`;
  const url = `/album/${id}`;
  return {
    title: `Album ${a.title}`,
    description,
    alternates: { canonical: url },
    openGraph: { type: "music.album", url, title: a.title, description, images: [{ url: cover || DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE }] },
    twitter: { card: "summary_large_image", title: a.title, description, images: [cover || DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE] },
  };
}

export default function Layout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return children;
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import SongCard from "@/components/music/SongCard";
import { usePlayer } from "@/components/music/PlayerProvider";
import Comments from "@/components/social/Comments";
import LikeButton from "@/components/social/LikeButton";
import ShareButtons from "@/components/social/ShareButtons";
import CoverImage from "@/components/ui/CoverImage";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { publicUrl } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { albumCoverPath, albumCoverUrl, deleteAlbum, getAlbum } from "@/services/albumService";
import { listAlbumSongs } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";
import { coverFileName } from "@/utils/download";
import { formatCount } from "@/utils/formatDate";

export default function AlbumPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const { playSong, playList, startRadio, current } = usePlayer();
  const [album, setAlbum] = useState<Album | null>(null);
  const [songs, setSongs] = useState<Song[]>([]);
  const [state, setState] = useState<"loading" | "ok" | "notfound">("loading");

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([getAlbum(id), listAlbumSongs(id)])
      .then(([a, s]) => {
        setAlbum(a);
        setSongs(s);
        setState(a ? "ok" : "notfound");
      })
      .catch(() => setState("notfound"));
  }, [id]);

  if (state === "loading") return <p className="py-16 text-center text-muted">Đang tải…</p>;
  if (!album) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-ink-soft">Không tìm thấy album.</p>;

  const solo = album.is_solo;
  const cover = albumCoverUrl(album);
  const coverPath = albumCoverPath(album);
  // Tải ảnh bìa: chỉ dành cho quản trị viên
  const coverDownload = isAdmin && coverPath ? publicUrl("covers", coverPath, coverFileName(album.title, coverPath)) : null;
  const canEdit = !!user && (user.id === album.owner_id || isAdmin);
  const totalPlays = songs.reduce((n, s) => n + s.play_count, 0);

  return (
    <div>
      <section className="hero-glow grid gap-6 rounded-3xl border border-line p-5 sm:rounded-[2rem] md:grid-cols-[16rem_1fr] md:gap-8 md:p-10">
        <div className="mx-auto w-52 md:w-full">
          <div className="aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-accent-soft shadow-xl">
            {cover ? (
              <CoverImage src={cover} alt={`Bìa ${album.title}`} eager className="h-full w-full" />
            ) : (
              // Chưa có ảnh bìa: dùng logo trang web
              // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
              <img src="/logo.png" alt="Phật Tại Tâm" className="h-full w-full object-contain p-6" />
            )}
          </div>
          {coverDownload && (
            <a href={coverDownload} download className="btn btn-secondary mt-3 w-full !min-h-0 !py-1.5 !text-xs">⬇ Tải ảnh bìa</a>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col justify-end">
          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">{solo ? "Single" : "Album"}</p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 font-serif text-3xl font-semibold leading-tight text-ink sm:text-4xl md:text-5xl">{album.title}</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted">
            <Link href={`/tac-gia/${album.owner_id}`} className="hover:underline">{album.owner?.full_name ?? ""}</Link>
            {" · "}{solo ? "Single" : `${songs.length} bài`} · ▶ {formatCount(totalPlays)} lượt nghe
          </p>
          {album.description && <p className="mt-4 max-w-2xl whitespace-pre-line text-ink-soft">{album.description}</p>}
          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">

            )}
            <LikeButton type="album" id={album.id} />

            )}
            {canEdit && !solo && (
              <>
                <Link href={`/album/sua/${album.id}`} className="btn btn-secondary">✏️ Sửa album</Link>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="btn btn-danger"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    if (confirm("Xoá album? Các bài hát vẫn được giữ lại (tự thành Single).")) {
                      await deleteAlbum(album);
                      router.push("/album");
                    }
                  }}
                >
                  Xoá
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-4"><ShareButtons title={album.title} /></div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-10">
        {!songs.length && (
          <p className="text-muted">
            {solo ? "Single này đang chờ duyệt." : "Album chưa có bài hát."}{" "}
            {canEdit && !solo && <>Vào <Link href="/tai-nhac" className="text-accent hover:underline">Tải nhạc</Link> hoặc sửa bài hát và chọn album này.</>}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-3">
          {songs.map((s, i) => (
            <SongCard key={s.id} song={s} trackNo={solo ? undefined : (s.track_no ?? i + 1)} active={current?.id === s.id} onPlay={() => playSong(s, songs, album.title)} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <Comments type="album" id={album.id} />
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import AlbumCard from "@/components/music/AlbumCard";
import { useAsync } from "@/hooks/useAsync";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { listAlbums } from "@/services/albumService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";

type Kind = "all" | "album" | "single";

export default function AlbumsPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { data: albums, loading, error } = useAsync<Album[]>(() => listAlbums({ includeSolo: true }), [], []);
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<Kind>("all");

  const shown = useMemo(
    () => albums.filter((a) => kind === "all" || (kind === "single" ? a.is_solo : !a.is_solo)),
    [albums, kind],
  );
  const counts = { all: albums.length, album: albums.filter((a) => !a.is_solo).length, single: albums.filter((a) => a.is_solo).length };

  const chip = (k: Kind, label: string) => (
    <button type="button" onClick={() => setKind(k)} className={`chip !px-3 !py-1 !text-sm ${kind === k ? "chip-on" : ""}`}>
      {label} <span className="opacity-60">{counts[k]}</span>
    </button>
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Âm nhạc</p>
      <div className="mb-6 mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-semibold text-ink sm:text-4xl">Album</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-ink-soft">Những tuyển tập nhạc và các Single — nghe trọn album hoặc bật radio theo từng album.</p>
        </div>
        {user && <Link href="/album/moi" className="btn btn-primary">＋ Tạo album</Link>}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {chip("all", "Tất cả")}
        {chip("album", "Album")}
        {chip("single", "Single")}
      </div>

      {loading && <p className="text-muted">Đang tải…</p>}
      {error && <p className="text-red-500">{error}</p>}
      {!loading && !shown.length && <p className="text-muted">Chưa có album nào.</p>}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3 sm:gap-6 lg:grid-cols-4">{shown.map((a) => <AlbumCard key={a.id} album={a} />)}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\app\api\media\delete\route.ts ===
import { parseKey, r2Delete } from "@/lib/r2";
import { authUser, jsonError } from "@/lib/serverAuth";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

/**
 * Xoá tệp khỏi R2. Body: { keys: ["images/<userId>/a.jpg", ...] }
 * Người dùng chỉ xoá được tệp trong thư mục của mình; admin xoá được mọi tệp.
 */
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const user = await authUser(req);
  if (!user) return jsonError("Bạn cần đăng nhập.", 401);

  let body: { keys?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonError("Dữ liệu không hợp lệ.");
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(body.keys)) return jsonError("Thiếu danh sách keys.");

  const allowed: string[] = [];
  let skipped = 0;
  for (const k of body.keys.slice(0, 500)) {
    const p = typeof k === "string" ? parseKey(k) : null;
    if (p && (user.isAdmin || p.owner === user.userId)) allowed.push(`${p.bucket}/${p.path}`);
    else skipped++;
  }

  try {
    const deleted = await r2Delete(allowed);
    return Response.json({ deleted, skipped });
  } catch (e) {
    return jsonError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Không xoá được.", 500);
  }
}

=== FILE: src\app\api\media\download\route.ts ===
import { parseKey, r2Url } from "@/lib/r2";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/**
 * Tải tệp về với tên đẹp: chuyển hướng tới URL R2 có header "attachment".
 * GET /api/media/download?key=music/<userId>/x.mp3&name=Ten%20bai%20hat.mp3
 * (Tệp vốn công khai nên không cần đăng nhập.)
 */
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const sp = new URL(req.url).searchParams;
  const key = sp.get("key") ?? "";
  const parsed = parseKey(key);
  if (!parsed) return new Response("Tệp không hợp lệ", { status: 400 });

  const base = parsed.path.split("/").pop() ?? "file";
  const fallback = base.replace(/^\d+-/, "");
  const name = (sp.get("name") || fallback).replace(/[\r\n"\\/]/g, "_").slice(0, 150);

  const disposition = `attachment; filename="${name.replace(/[^\x20-\x7e]/g, "_")}"; filename*=UTF-8''${encodeURIComponent(name)}`;
  try {
    const url = r2Url("GET", key, { query: { "response-content-disposition": disposition }, expires: 300 });
    return Response.redirect(url, 302);
  } catch {
    return new Response("Chưa cấu hình R2", { status: 500 });
  }
}

=== FILE: src\app\api\media\list\route.ts ===
import { isBucket, r2List } from "@/lib/r2";
import { authUser, jsonError } from "@/lib/serverAuth";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** Liệt kê tệp trong một bucket (chỉ admin) — dùng cho trang Dọn dẹp */
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const user = await authUser(req);
  if (!user?.isAdmin) return jsonError("Chỉ quản trị viên được xem.", 403);

  const bucket = new URL(req.url).searchParams.get("bucket");
  if (!isBucket(bucket)) return jsonError("bucket không hợp lệ.");

  try {
    const objects = await r2List(`${bucket}/`);
    return Response.json({
      files: objects.map((o) => ({
        key: o.key,
        path: o.key.slice(bucket.length + 1),
        size: o.size,
        lastModified: o.lastModified,
      })),
    });
  } catch (e) {
    return jsonError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Không liệt kê được.", 500);
  }
}

=== FILE: src\app\api\media\upload-url\route.ts ===
import { ALLOWED_TYPES, MAX_BYTES, isBucket, r2Url } from "@/lib/r2";
import { authUser, jsonError } from "@/lib/serverAuth";
import { safeFileName } from "@/utils/slug";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

/**
 * Xin URL tải lên thẳng Cloudflare R2 (trình duyệt PUT trực tiếp — không đi qua máy chủ Vercel).
 * Body: { bucket, filename, size?, prefix? }
 */
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const user = await authUser(req);
  if (!user) return jsonError("Bạn cần đăng nhập.", 401);

  let body: { bucket?: unknown; filename?: unknown; size?: unknown; prefix?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonError("Dữ liệu không hợp lệ.");
  }

  const { bucket, filename } = body;
  if (!isBucket(bucket) || typeof filename !== "string" || !filename) return jsonError("Thiếu bucket hoặc tên tệp.");

  const ext = filename.includes(".") ? filename.split(".").pop()!.toLowerCase() : "";
  const contentType = ALLOWED_TYPES[bucket][ext];
  if (!contentType) {
    return jsonError(`Định dạng .${ext || "?"} không được phép. Cho phép: ${Object.keys(ALLOWED_TYPES[bucket]).join(", ")}.`);
  }

  const size = typeof body.size === "number" ? body.size : 0;
  if (size > MAX_BYTES[bucket]) return jsonError(`Tệp vượt quá ${Math.round(MAX_BYTES[bucket] / 1024 / 1024)} MB.`);

  const prefix = typeof body.prefix === "string" ? body.prefix.replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/gi, "").slice(0, 20) : "";
  const path = `${user.userId}/${prefix}${Date.now()}-${safeFileName(filename)}`;
  const key = `${bucket}/${path}`;

  try {
    const uploadUrl = r2Url("PUT", key, { headers: { "content-type": contentType }, expires: 900 });
    return Response.json({ path, key, uploadUrl, contentType });
  } catch (e) {
    return jsonError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Lỗi cấu hình R2.", 500);
  }
}

=== FILE: src\app\bai-hat\[id]\layout.tsx ===
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE } from "@/lib/site";
import { serverPublicUrl, serverSupabase } from "@/lib/supabaseServer";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

/** Supabase có thể trả quan hệ dạng object hoặc mảng — lấy phần tử đầu */
function one<T>(v: unknown): T | null {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return (v[0] as T) ?? null;
  return (v as T) ?? null;
}

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { data: s } = await serverSupabase()
    .from("songs")
    .select("title, artist, lyrics, cover_path, album_rel:albums(title, cover_path)")
    .eq("id", id)
    .eq("is_published", true)
    .maybeSingle();
  if (!s) return { title: "Không tìm thấy bài hát" };

  const album = one<{ title: string; cover_path: string | null }>(s.album_rel);
  const cover = s.cover_path ? serverPublicUrl("covers", s.cover_path) : album?.cover_path ? serverPublicUrl("covers", album.cover_path) : null;
  const title = `${s.title}${s.artist ? ` — ${s.artist}` : ""}`;
  const description =
    (s.lyrics ? String(s.lyrics).replace(/\[[^\]]*\]/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 160) : "") ||
    `Nghe “${s.title}”${album ? ` trong album ${album.title}` : ""} trên Phật Tại Tâm.`;
  const url = `/bai-hat/${id}`;

  return {
    title,
    description,
    alternates: { canonical: url },
    openGraph: { type: "music.song", url, title, description, images: [{ url: cover || DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE }] },
    twitter: { card: "summary_large_image", title, description, images: [cover || DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE] },
  };
}

export default function Layout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return children;
}

=== FILE: src\app\bai-viet\[slug]\layout.tsx ===
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE } from "@/lib/site";
import { serverSupabase } from "@/lib/supabaseServer";
import { firstImage, plainExcerpt } from "@/utils/html";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> };

/** Supabase có thể trả quan hệ dạng object hoặc mảng — lấy phần tử đầu */
function one<T>(v: unknown): T | null {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return (v[0] as T) ?? null;
  return (v as T) ?? null;
}

/** Thẻ SEO + ảnh/tiêu đề khi chia sẻ link qua Facebook, Zalo… */
export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const { data: post } = await serverSupabase()
    .from("posts")
    .select("title, excerpt, content, cover_image, tags, author_name, created_at, updated_at, author:profiles(full_name)")
    .eq("slug", decodeURIComponent(slug))
    .eq("is_published", true)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (!post) return { title: "Không tìm thấy bài viết" };

  const tags: string[] = Array.isArray(post.tags) ? post.tags : [];
  const description = post.excerpt || plainExcerpt(post.content, 180) || "Bài viết trên Phật Tại Tâm.";
  const image = post.cover_image || firstImage(post.content);
  const author = post.author_name || one<{ full_name: string | null }>(post.author)?.full_name || undefined;
  const url = `/bai-viet/${slug}`;

  return {
    title: post.title,
    description,
    keywords: tags,
    alternates: { canonical: url },
    authors: author ? [{ name: author }] : undefined,
    openGraph: {
      type: "article",
      url,
      title: post.title,
      description,
      publishedTime: post.created_at,
      modifiedTime: post.updated_at,
      authors: author ? [author] : undefined,
      tags,
      images: [{ url: image || DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE }],
    },
    twitter: { card: "summary_large_image", title: post.title, description, images: [image || DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE] },
  };
}

export default function Layout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return children;
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import RequireAuth from "@/components/auth/RequireAuth";
import Avatar from "@/components/social/Avatar";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { deleteAlbum, listAlbumsByOwner } from "@/services/albumService";
import { deletePost, listMyPosts } from "@/services/postService";
import { deleteSong, listMySongs } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";
import type { Post } from "@/types/post";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";
import { formatCount, formatDateTime, wasEdited } from "@/utils/formatDate";

function Status({ ok }: { ok: boolean }) {
  return ok ? (
    <span className="rounded-full bg-green-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-green-800">Đã đăng</span>
  ) : (
    <span className="rounded-full bg-yellow-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-yellow-800">Chờ duyệt</span>
  );
}

function Times({ created, updated, verb = "Đăng" }: { created: string; updated: string; verb?: string }) {
  return (
    <>
      {verb}: {formatDateTime(created)}
      {wasEdited(created, updated) && <> · Sửa: {formatDateTime(updated)}</>}
    </>
  );
}

function Dashboard() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<Post[]>([]);
  const [songs, setSongs] = useState<Song[]>([]);
  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<Album[]>([]);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const [p, s, a] = await Promise.all([listMyPosts(user.id), listMySongs(user.id), listAlbumsByOwner(user.id)]);
    setPosts(p);
    setSongs(s);
    setAlbums(a);
  }, [user]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSent(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has("sent"));
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const box = "divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface";
  const views = posts.reduce((n, p) => n + p.view_count, 0);
  const plays = songs.reduce((n, s) => n + s.play_count, 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-12">
      <section className="hero-glow rounded-[2rem] border border-line px-8 py-10">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-5">
          <Avatar url={profile?.avatar_url} name={profile?.full_name} size={72} />
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Tài khoản</p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">Xin chào, {profile?.full_name ?? user?.email} 🙏</h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">👁 {formatCount(views)} lượt đọc · ▶ {formatCount(plays)} lượt nghe</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {sent && <p className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-green-100 p-3 text-green-800">Đã lưu! Nội dung đang chờ duyệt.</p>}
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <Link href="/viet-bai" className="btn btn-primary">✍️ Viết bài / tải sách</Link>
          <Link href="/tai-nhac" className="btn btn-secondary">⬆ Tải nhạc</Link>
          <Link href="/album/moi" className="btn btn-secondary">💿 Tạo album</Link>
          <Link href="/playlist" className="btn btn-secondary">♫ Playlist</Link>
          <Link href="/ho-so" className="btn btn-secondary">👤 Hồ sơ</Link>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-ink">Bài viết của tôi ({posts.length})</h2>
        <div className={box}>
          {posts.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/bai-viet/${p.slug}`} className="font-medium text-ink hover:underline">{p.title}</Link>
                <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-muted">{p.file_name && "📎 · "}👁 {formatCount(p.view_count)}</span>
                {p.tags.length > 0 && <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">{p.tags.map((t) => <span key={t} className="chip">#{t}</span>)}</div>}
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted"><Times created={p.created_at} updated={p.updated_at} /></p>
              </div>
              <Status ok={p.is_published} />
              <Link href={`/sua-bai/${p.id}`} className="btn btn-secondary !py-1.5">✏️ Sửa</Link>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger !py-1.5"
                onClick={async () => { if (confirm("Xoá bài này (kèm tệp đính kèm và ảnh trong bài)?")) { await deletePost(p); load(); } }}>Xoá</button>
            </div>
          ))}
          {!posts.length && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted">Chưa có bài viết.</p>}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-ink">Album của tôi ({albums.length})</h2>
        <div className={box}>
          {albums.map((a) => (
            <div key={a.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/album/${a.id}`} className="font-medium text-ink hover:underline">💿 {a.title}</Link>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted"><Times created={a.created_at} updated={a.updated_at} verb="Tạo" /></p>
              </div>
              <Link href={`/album/sua/${a.id}`} className="btn btn-secondary !py-1.5">✏️ Sửa</Link>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger !py-1.5"
                onClick={async () => { if (confirm("Xoá album? Bài hát vẫn được giữ lại.")) { await deleteAlbum(a); load(); } }}>Xoá</button>
            </div>
          ))}
          {!albums.length && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted">Chưa có album.</p>}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-ink">Nhạc của tôi ({songs.length})</h2>
        <div className={box}>
          {songs.map((s) => (
            <div key={s.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/bai-hat/${s.id}`} className="font-medium text-ink hover:underline">{s.title}</Link>
                <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-muted">
                  {s.artist}{s.album_info && ` · ${s.album_info.title}`} · ▶ {formatCount(s.play_count)}{s.lyrics ? " · có lời" : ""}
                </span>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted"><Times created={s.created_at} updated={s.updated_at} verb="Tải lên" /></p>
              </div>
              <Status ok={s.is_published} />
              <Link href={`/sua-nhac/${s.id}`} className="btn btn-secondary !py-1.5">✏️ Sửa</Link>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger !py-1.5"
                onClick={async () => { if (confirm("Xoá bài hát này (kèm tệp nhạc và ảnh bìa)?")) { await deleteSong(s); load(); } }}>Xoá</button>
            </div>
          ))}
          {!songs.length && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted">Chưa có bài hát.</p>}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function DashboardPage() {
  return <RequireAuth><Dashboard /></RequireAuth>;
}

=== FILE: src\app\ho-so\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import RequireAuth from "@/components/auth/RequireAuth";
import Avatar from "@/components/social/Avatar";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { updateMyProfile } from "@/services/profileService";
import { removeImageUrls, uploadImage } from "@/services/storage";

function ProfileForm() {
  const { user, profile, refreshProfile } = useAuth();
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [bio, setBio] = useState("");
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  /** Ảnh đại diện đã tải lên nhưng chưa lưu */
  const pending = useRef<string[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setName(profile?.full_name ?? "");
    setBio(profile?.bio ?? "");
    setAvatar(profile?.avatar_url ?? null);
  }, [profile]);

  async function pickAvatar(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const f = e.target.files?.[0];
    e.target.value = "";
    if (!f || !user) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg(null);
    try {
      const url = await uploadImage(f, user.id, "avatar-");
      pending.current.push(url);
      setAvatar(url);
    } catch (err) {
      setMsg({ ok: false, text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Không tải được ảnh" });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function save(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!user) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg(null);
    const oldAvatar = profile?.avatar_url ?? null;
    try {
      await updateMyProfile(user.id, { full_name: name, bio, avatar_url: avatar });
      // Xoá ảnh cũ (nếu đã đổi/bỏ) và các ảnh thử nhưng không chọn
      await removeImageUrls([...(oldAvatar !== avatar ? [oldAvatar] : []), ...pending.current.filter((u) => u !== avatar)]);
      pending.current = [];
      await refreshProfile();
      setMsg({ ok: true, text: "Đã lưu hồ sơ." });
    } catch (err) {
      setMsg({ ok: false, text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Không lưu được" });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function discard() {
    await removeImageUrls(pending.current);
    pending.current = [];
    setAvatar(profile?.avatar_url ?? null);
    setName(profile?.full_name ?? "");
    setBio(profile?.bio ?? "");
    setMsg(null);
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={save} className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Tài khoản</p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">Hồ sơ của tôi</h1>
        </div>
        {user && <Link href={`/tac-gia/${user.id}`} className="text-sm text-accent hover:underline">Xem trang công khai →</Link>}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-5 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5">
        <Avatar url={avatar} name={name} size={88} />
        <div className="space-y-2 text-sm">
          <label className="inline-block cursor-pointer rounded-full border border-line px-4 py-2 text-ink hover:bg-soft">
            {busy ? "Đang tải…" : "📷 Đổi ảnh đại diện"}
            <input type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={pickAvatar} />
          </label>
          {avatar && <button type="button" onClick={() => setAvatar(null)} className="ml-2 text-red-600 hover:underline">Bỏ ảnh</button>}
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">Ảnh vuông, tối đa 10 MB. Ảnh cũ sẽ được xoá khi bạn bấm Lưu.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <label className="block">
        <span className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-ink">Tên hiển thị</span>
        <input required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} className="field" maxLength={80} />
      </label>
      <label className="block">
        <span className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-ink">Giới thiệu</span>
        <textarea value={bio} onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)} rows={5} maxLength={1000} className="field" placeholder="Vài dòng về bạn…" />
      </label>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted">Email: {user?.email}</p>

      {msg && <p className={msg.ok ? "text-green-600" : "text-red-600"}>{msg.text}</p>}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <button disabled={busy} className="rounded-full bg-ink px-7 py-3 font-medium text-paper disabled:opacity-50">Lưu hồ sơ</button>
        <button type="button" onClick={discard} className="rounded-full border border-line px-6 py-3 text-ink-soft hover:bg-soft">Huỷ thay đổi</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

export default function ProfilePage() {
  return <RequireAuth><ProfileForm /></RequireAuth>;
}

import { Noto_Sans, Noto_Serif } from "next/font/google";

=== FILE: src\app\login\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { resetPassword, signInWithEmail, signInWithGoogle, signUpWithEmail } from "@/lib/auth";
import { TERMS_CHECKBOX_TEXT } from "@/lib/terms";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";

type Mode = "login" | "signup";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("login");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [agree, setAgree] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ type: "ok" | "err"; text: string } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && user) router.replace("/dashboard");
  }, [user, loading, router]);

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (mode === "signup" && !agree) return setMsg({ type: "err", text: "Bạn cần đồng ý Điều lệ để tạo tài khoản." });
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg(null);
    try {
      if (mode === "login") {
        await signInWithEmail(email, password);
        router.replace("/dashboard");
      } else {
        const needConfirm = await signUpWithEmail(email, password, name);
        setMsg({ type: "ok", text: needConfirm ? "Đăng ký thành công! Vui lòng kiểm tra email để xác nhận tài khoản." : "Đăng ký thành công!" });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setMsg({ type: "err", text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Có lỗi xảy ra" });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleReset() {
    if (!email) return setMsg({ type: "err", text: "Nhập email trước rồi bấm Quên mật khẩu." });
    try {
      await resetPassword(email);
      setMsg({ type: "ok", text: "Đã gửi email đặt lại mật khẩu." });
    } catch (err) {
      setMsg({ type: "err", text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Có lỗi xảy ra" });
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="hero-glow mx-auto max-w-md rounded-[2rem] border border-line p-8 shadow-xl">
      <div className="text-center">
        {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
        <img src="/logo-sm.png" alt="Phật Tại Tâm" width={72} height={72} className="mx-auto h-[72px] w-[72px] rounded-full" />
        <h1 className="mt-4 font-serif text-3xl font-semibold text-ink">{mode === "login" ? "Chào mừng trở lại" : "Tạo tài khoản"}</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">Thư Viện Số Phật Tại Tâm</p>
      </div>

      <button type="button" onClick={() => signInWithGoogle().catch((e) => setMsg({ type: "err", text: e.message }))}
        className="btn btn-primary mt-8 w-full !py-3">
        <span className="font-bold">G</span> Tiếp tục với Google
      </button>
      <p className="mt-2 text-center text-xs text-muted">Lần đầu đăng nhập, bạn sẽ được mời đọc và đồng ý <Link href="/dieu-le" target="_blank" className="text-accent hover:underline">Điều lệ</Link>.</p>

      <div className="my-6 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted">
        <div className="h-px flex-1 bg-line" /> hoặc dùng email <div className="h-px flex-1 bg-line" />
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
        {mode === "signup" && <input required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Họ và tên" className="field" />}
        <input required type="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="Email" className="field" />
        <input required type="password" minLength={6} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="Mật khẩu (ít nhất 6 ký tự)" className="field" />

        {mode === "signup" && (
          <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface/60 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">
            <input type="checkbox" required checked={agree} onChange={(e) => setAgree(e.target.checked)} className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 accent-[var(--accent)]" />
            <span>
              {TERMS_CHECKBOX_TEXT}{" "}
              <Link href="/dieu-le" target="_blank" className="font-medium text-accent hover:underline">Đọc toàn văn Điều lệ →</Link>
            </span>
          </label>
        )}

        <button disabled={busy || (mode === "signup" && !agree)} className="btn btn-primary w-full !py-3">
          {busy ? "Đang xử lý…" : mode === "login" ? "Đăng nhập" : "Đăng ký"}
        </button>
      </form>

      {msg && <p className={`mt-4 text-sm ${msg.type === "ok" ? "text-green-500" : "text-red-500"}`}>{msg.text}</p>}

      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-between text-sm">
        <button type="button" className="text-accent hover:underline" onClick={() => { setMode(mode === "login" ? "signup" : "login"); setMsg(null); }}>
          {mode === "login" ? "Chưa có tài khoản? Đăng ký" : "Đã có tài khoản? Đăng nhập"}
        </button>
        {mode === "login" && <button type="button" onClick={handleReset} className="text-muted hover:underline">Quên mật khẩu</button>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\app\nhac\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import AlbumCard from "@/components/music/AlbumCard";
import { usePlayer } from "@/components/music/PlayerProvider";
import SongCard from "@/components/music/SongCard";
import { RadioIcon } from "@/components/ui/Icons";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { useSongs } from "@/hooks/useSongs";
import { listAlbums } from "@/services/albumService";
import { loadRadioQueue } from "@/services/radioService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";
import { matchSearch } from "@/utils/slug";

export default function MusicPage() {
  const { songs, loading, error } = useSongs();
  const { user, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const { playSong, startRadio, current } = usePlayer();
  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<Album[]>([]);
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [radioAlbum, setRadioAlbum] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<"new" | "plays">("new");
  const [radioBusy, setRadioBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Gồm cả Single (album solo)
    listAlbums({ includeSolo: true }).then(setAlbums).catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  // Radio theo album: chỉ album thật (radio một bài Single không có ý nghĩa)
  const realAlbums = useMemo(() => albums.filter((a) => !a.is_solo), [albums]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const list = songs.filter((s) => matchSearch(`${s.title} ${s.artist ?? ""} ${s.album_info?.title ?? ""}`, q));
    return sort === "plays" ? [...list].sort((a, b) => b.play_count - a.play_count) : list;
  }, [songs, q, sort]);

  async function radio() {
    const album = realAlbums.find((a) => a.id === radioAlbum);
    if (album) {
      startRadio(songs.filter((s) => s.album_id === album.id), `Radio · ${album.title}`);
      return;
    }
    setRadioBusy(true);
    try {
      const r = await loadRadioQueue();
      startRadio(r.songs, "Radio Phật Tại Tâm", r.shuffle);
    } finally {
      setRadioBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <section className="hero-glow mb-10 grid items-center gap-6 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-line px-5 py-8 sm:mb-12 sm:rounded-[2rem] sm:px-8 sm:py-10 md:grid-cols-[1.3fr_1fr] md:px-12">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-accent">Âm nhạc</p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 font-serif text-3xl font-semibold text-ink sm:text-4xl">Nghe trong lúc đọc</h1>
          <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-sm text-ink-soft sm:text-base">Nghe, tải về, xem lời, hoặc bật Radio Phật Tại Tâm — phát liên tục từ danh sách do ban quản trị tuyển chọn.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 md:items-end">
          <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row md:justify-end">
            <select value={radioAlbum} onChange={(e) => setRadioAlbum(e.target.value)} className="field !rounded-full !py-2.5 text-sm sm:!w-auto">
              <option value="">Radio Phật Tại Tâm</option>
              {realAlbums.map((a) => <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>Radio album: {a.title}</option>)}
            </select>
            <button type="button" onClick={radio} disabled={!songs.length || radioBusy} className="btn btn-primary">
              <RadioIcon size={18} /> {radioBusy ? "Đang tải…" : "Bật Radio"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-x-4 gap-y-2 text-sm md:justify-end">
            {user && (
              <>
                <Link href="/tai-nhac" className="text-accent hover:underline">⬆ Tải nhạc lên</Link>
                <Link href="/album/moi" className="text-accent hover:underline">＋ Tạo album</Link>
                <Link href="/playlist" className="text-accent hover:underline">♫ Playlist của tôi</Link>
              </>
            )}
            {isAdmin && <Link href="/admin/radio" className="text-accent hover:underline">⚙ Quản lý Radio</Link>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {albums.length > 0 && (
        <section className="mb-10 sm:mb-12">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-end justify-between sm:mb-5">
            <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-ink">Album &amp; Single</h2>
            <Link href="/album" className="text-sm font-medium text-accent hover:underline">Tất cả →</Link>
          </div>
          {/* Điện thoại: cuộn ngang; máy tính: lưới */}
          <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-4 flex snap-x gap-4 overflow-x-auto px-4 sm:mx-0 sm:grid sm:grid-cols-4 sm:gap-5 sm:overflow-visible sm:px-0 lg:grid-cols-6">
            {albums.slice(0, 12).map((a) => (
              <div key={a.id} className="w-36 shrink-0 snap-start sm:w-auto"><AlbumCard album={a} /></div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 sm:mb-5">
        <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-ink">Tất cả bài hát</h2>
        <select value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as "new" | "plays")} className="field !w-auto !rounded-full !py-1.5 text-sm">
          <option value="new">Mới nhất</option>
          <option value="plays">Nghe nhiều</option>
        </select>
        <input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="🔍 Tìm bài hát, ca sĩ, album…" className="field !w-full !rounded-full sm:ml-auto sm:!w-72" />
      </div>

      {loading && <p className="text-muted">Đang tải…</p>}
      {error && <p className="text-red-500">{error}</p>}
      {!loading && !filtered.length && <p className="text-muted">Chưa có bài hát nào.</p>}
      <div className="grid gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 lg:grid-cols-2">
        {filtered.map((s) => (
          <SongCard key={s.id} song={s} active={current?.id === s.id} onPlay={() => playSong(s, filtered, "Thư viện nhạc")} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\app\og\route.tsx ===
import { ImageResponse } from "next/og";
import { SITE_URL } from "@/lib/site";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const size = { width: 1200, height: 630 };

/* Ảnh chia sẻ mặc định (khi bài viết không có ảnh): logo + tên website.
   Chỉ dùng chữ không dấu vì font mặc định của trình tạo ảnh chưa hỗ trợ đủ dấu tiếng Việt. */
export async function GET() {
  let logo: string | null = null;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${SITE_URL}/logo.png`);
    if (res.ok) logo = `data:image/png;base64,${Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer()).toString("base64")}`;
  } catch {}

  return new ImageResponse(
    (
      <div
        style={{
          width: "100%",
          height: "100%",
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          background: "linear-gradient(135deg, #0d0b08 0%, #221c14 60%, #3a2c1a 100%)",
          color: "#f4ecdc",
        }}
      >
        {logo ? (
          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
          <img src={logo} width={300} height={300} alt="" style={{ borderRadius: 150 }} />
        ) : (
          <div style={{ width: 200, height: 200, borderRadius: 100, background: "#e2b04a", color: "#1c1408", fontSize: 130, fontWeight: 700, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>P</div>
        )}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 36, fontSize: 44, letterSpacing: 12, color: "#e2b04a" }}>PHATTAITAM.COM</div>
      </div>
    ),
    { ...size, headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=86400, s-maxage=86400" } },
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import AlbumCard from "@/components/music/AlbumCard";
import { usePlayer } from "@/components/music/PlayerProvider";
import BookCard from "@/components/posts/BookCard";

import { listAlbums } from "@/services/albumService";
import { loadRadioQueue } from "@/services/radioService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";

export default function HomePage() {

    setRadioBusy(true);

    } finally {
      setRadioBusy(false);
    }

    <div className="space-y-12 sm:space-y-20">
      <section className="hero-glow grid items-center gap-8 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-line px-5 py-10 sm:rounded-[2rem] sm:px-6 sm:py-14 md:grid-cols-[1.3fr_1fr] md:px-12">
        <div className="order-2 text-center md:order-1 md:text-left">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-accent sm:text-xs sm:tracking-[0.25em]">{SITE_TAGLINE}</p>
          <h1 className="mt-4 font-serif text-4xl font-semibold leading-tight text-ink sm:text-5xl md:text-6xl">
            Đọc. Nghe.
            <br />
            <span className="italic text-accent">Sáng tạo.</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-lg text-base text-ink-soft sm:mt-5 sm:text-lg md:mx-0">
            Thư viện số nơi sách, âm nhạc, ý tưởng và sáng tác của cộng đồng cùng hội tụ.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:mt-8 sm:flex-row sm:justify-center md:justify-start">
            <Link href="/thu-vien" className="btn btn-primary !px-6 !py-3">Khám phá thư viện →</Link>
            <Link href="/nhac" className="btn btn-secondary !px-6 !py-3">Mở Nhạc</Link>
          </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-ink sm:text-3xl">{posts.length}</dt>
              <dd className="text-xs text-muted sm:text-sm">bài viết &amp; sách</dd>
            </div>

              <dd className="text-xs text-muted sm:text-sm">ý tưởng</dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
        </div>

          <div className="absolute -inset-6 rounded-full bg-accent/20 blur-3xl" />
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}

        </div>
      </section>

        {sectionHead("Sách", "Sách nổi bật", "/sach", "Xem tất cả →")}
        {loading ? (
          <p className="text-muted">Đang tải…</p>
        ) : featured.length ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:gap-6 md:grid-cols-4">{featured.map((p) => <BookCard key={p.id} post={p} />)}</div>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-muted">Chưa có sách nào.</p>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="hero-glow grid items-center gap-6 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-line px-5 py-8 sm:rounded-[2rem] sm:px-8 sm:py-12 md:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1fr] md:px-12">

        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
