=== FILE: src\app\playlist\[id]\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import SongCard from "@/components/music/SongCard";
import { usePlayer } from "@/components/music/PlayerProvider";
import ShareButtons from "@/components/social/ShareButtons";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { getPlaylist, listPlaylistSongs, removeSongFromPlaylist, updatePlaylist } from "@/services/playlistService";
import type { Playlist } from "@/types/social";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";

export default function PlaylistPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { playSong, playList, startRadio, current } = usePlayer();
  const [pl, setPl] = useState<Playlist | null>(null);
  const [songs, setSongs] = useState<Song[]>([]);
  const [state, setState] = useState<"loading" | "ok" | "notfound">("loading");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [p, s] = await Promise.all([getPlaylist(id), listPlaylistSongs(id)]);
      setPl(p);
      setSongs(s);
      setState(p ? "ok" : "notfound");
    } catch {
      setState("notfound");
    }
  }, [id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  if (state === "loading") return <p className="py-16 text-center text-muted">Đang tải…</p>;
  if (!pl) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-ink-soft">Không tìm thấy playlist (hoặc playlist đang để riêng tư).</p>;

  const mine = user?.id === pl.owner_id;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Playlist</p>
      <h1 className="mt-2 font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">{pl.title}</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">
        <Link href={`/tac-gia/${pl.owner_id}`} className="hover:underline">{pl.owner?.full_name ?? ""}</Link> · {songs.length} bài · {pl.is_public ? "Công khai" : "Riêng tư"}
      </p>
      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <button type="button" disabled={!songs.length} onClick={() => playList(songs, pl.title)}
          className="rounded-full bg-ink px-6 py-3 font-medium text-paper disabled:opacity-50">▶ Phát</button>
        <button type="button" disabled={!songs.length} onClick={() => startRadio(songs, `Radio · ${pl.title}`)}
          className="rounded-full border border-line bg-surface px-5 py-3 font-medium text-ink hover:bg-soft disabled:opacity-50">🔀 Phát ngẫu nhiên</button>
        {mine && (
          <>
            <button type="button" className="rounded-full border border-line px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink hover:bg-soft"
              onClick={async () => { const t = prompt("Tên playlist:", pl.title); if (t?.trim()) { await updatePlaylist(pl.id, { title: t.trim() }); load(); } }}>
              ✏️ Đổi tên
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="rounded-full border border-line px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink hover:bg-soft"
              onClick={async () => { await updatePlaylist(pl.id, { is_public: !pl.is_public }); load(); }}>
              {pl.is_public ? "🔒 Chuyển riêng tư" : "🌐 Công khai"}
            </button>
          </>
        )}
        {pl.is_public && <ShareButtons title={pl.title} />}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8 space-y-3">
        {!songs.length && <p className="text-muted">Playlist trống. Vào <Link href="/nhac" className="text-accent hover:underline">Nhạc</Link> và bấm ＋ để thêm bài.</p>}
        {songs.map((s, i) => (
          <SongCard key={s.id} song={s} trackNo={i + 1} active={current?.id === s.id} onPlay={() => playSong(s, songs, pl.title)}
            extra={mine ? (
              <button type="button" title="Bỏ khỏi playlist" aria-label="Bỏ khỏi playlist"
                className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-line text-red-600 hover:bg-soft"
                onClick={async () => { await removeSongFromPlaylist(pl.id, s.id); load(); }}>−</button>
            ) : undefined} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\app\playlist\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import RequireAuth from "@/components/auth/RequireAuth";
import { useAsync } from "@/hooks/useAsync";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { createPlaylist, deletePlaylist, listMyPlaylists } from "@/services/playlistService";
import type { Playlist } from "@/types/social";
import { formatDate } from "@/utils/formatDate";

function MyPlaylists() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { data: lists, reload } = useAsync<Playlist[]>(() => listMyPlaylists(user!.id), [user?.id], []);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [isPublic, setIsPublic] = useState(true);

  async function create(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim() || !user) return;
    await createPlaylist(title, user.id, isPublic);
    setTitle("");
    reload();
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl">
      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Âm nhạc</p>
      <h1 className="mt-2 font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">Playlist của tôi</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-ink-soft">Bấm nút ＋ trên mỗi bài hát để thêm vào playlist.</p>

      <form onSubmit={create} className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <input value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="Tên playlist mới" className="field !w-auto flex-1" />
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-ink-soft">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={isPublic} onChange={(e) => setIsPublic(e.target.checked)} /> Công khai
        </label>
        <button className="rounded-full bg-ink px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-paper">＋ Tạo</button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-8 divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface">
        {lists.map((p) => (
          <div key={p.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4">
            <span className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-accent-soft text-xl">♫</span>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <Link href={`/playlist/${p.id}`} className="font-medium text-ink hover:underline">{p.title}</Link>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted">{p.is_public ? "Công khai" : "Riêng tư"} · tạo {formatDate(p.created_at)}</p>
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="text-sm text-red-600 hover:underline"
              onClick={async () => { if (confirm("Xoá playlist này?")) { await deletePlaylist(p.id); reload(); } }}>Xoá</button>
          </div>
        ))}
        {!lists.length && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted">Chưa có playlist nào.</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function PlaylistsPage() {
  return <RequireAuth><MyPlaylists /></RequireAuth>;
}

=== FILE: src\app\sitemap.ts ===
import type { MetadataRoute } from "next";
import { SITE_URL } from "@/lib/site";
import { serverSupabase } from "@/lib/supabaseServer";

export const revalidate = 3600;

export default async function sitemap(): Promise<MetadataRoute.Sitemap> {
  const sb = serverSupabase();
  const fixed = ["", "/thu-vien", "/sach", "/nhac", "/album", "/dieu-le"].map((p) => ({
    url: `${SITE_URL}${p}`,
    changeFrequency: "daily" as const,
    priority: p === "" ? 1 : 0.8,
  }));

  const [posts, songs, albums] = await Promise.all([
    sb.from("posts").select("slug, updated_at").eq("is_published", true).limit(5000),
    sb.from("songs").select("id, updated_at").eq("is_published", true).limit(5000),
    sb.from("albums").select("id, updated_at").limit(1000),
  ]);

  return [
    ...fixed,
    ...(posts.data ?? []).map((p: { slug: string; updated_at: string }) => ({ url: `${SITE_URL}/bai-viet/${p.slug}`, lastModified: p.updated_at, priority: 0.7 })),
    ...(songs.data ?? []).map((s: { id: string; updated_at: string }) => ({ url: `${SITE_URL}/bai-hat/${s.id}`, lastModified: s.updated_at, priority: 0.6 })),
    ...(albums.data ?? []).map((a: { id: string; updated_at: string }) => ({ url: `${SITE_URL}/album/${a.id}`, lastModified: a.updated_at, priority: 0.6 })),
  ];
}

=== FILE: src\app\sua-bai\[id]\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import RequireAuth from "@/components/auth/RequireAuth";
import PostForm from "@/components/posts/PostForm";
import { useAsync } from "@/hooks/useAsync";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { getPostById, updatePost } from "@/services/postService";
import { formatDateTime, wasEdited } from "@/utils/formatDate";

function Edit() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const { data: post, loading } = useAsync(() => getPostById(id), [id], null);

  if (loading) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-muted">Đang tải…</p>;
  if (!post) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-ink-soft">Không tìm thấy bài viết.</p>;
  if (!user || (user.id !== post.author_id && !isAdmin)) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-ink-soft">Bạn không có quyền sửa bài này.</p>;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-4">
      <Link href="/dashboard" className="text-sm text-accent hover:underline">← Tài khoản</Link>
      <h1 className="font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">Sửa bài viết</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted">
        Đăng lúc {formatDateTime(post.created_at)}
        {wasEdited(post.created_at, post.updated_at) && ` · Sửa lần cuối ${formatDateTime(post.updated_at)}`}
      </p>
      {!isAdmin && post.is_published && (
        <p className="rounded-xl bg-accent-soft p-3 text-sm text-ink-soft">Sau khi lưu, bài sẽ chuyển về trạng thái <b>Chờ duyệt</b>.</p>
      )}
      <PostForm
        post={post}
        submitLabel="Lưu thay đổi"
        onCancel={() => router.back()}
        onSubmit={async (input) => {
          const saved = await updatePost(post, input, user.id, isAdmin);
          router.push(isAdmin ? `/bai-viet/${saved.slug}` : "/dashboard?sent=1");
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function EditPostPage() {
  return <RequireAuth><Edit /></RequireAuth>;
}

import Link from "next/link";

=== FILE: src\app\tac-gia\[id]\layout.tsx ===
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE } from "@/lib/site";
import { serverSupabase } from "@/lib/supabaseServer";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { data: p } = await serverSupabase().from("profiles").select("full_name, bio, avatar_url").eq("id", id).maybeSingle();
  if (!p) return { title: "Không tìm thấy tác giả" };
  const name = p.full_name ?? "Tác giả";
  const description = p.bio?.slice(0, 180) || `Bài viết và âm nhạc của ${name} trên Phật Tại Tâm.`;
  return {
    title: name,
    description,
    alternates: { canonical: `/tac-gia/${id}` },
    openGraph: { type: "profile", title: name, description, images: [{ url: p.avatar_url || DEFAULT_OG_IMAGE }] },
  };
}

export default function Layout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return children;
}

=== FILE: src\app\tac-gia\[id]\page.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import AlbumCard from "@/components/music/AlbumCard";
import SongCard from "@/components/music/SongCard";
import { usePlayer } from "@/components/music/PlayerProvider";
import PostCard from "@/components/posts/PostCard";
import Avatar from "@/components/social/Avatar";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { listAlbumsByOwner } from "@/services/albumService";
import { listPostsByAuthor } from "@/services/postService";
import { getPublicProfile } from "@/services/profileService";
import { listSongsByUploader } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";
import type { Post } from "@/types/post";
import type { Profile } from "@/types/profile";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";
import { formatDate } from "@/utils/formatDate";

export default function AuthorPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { playSong, current } = usePlayer();
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<Post[]>([]);
  const [songs, setSongs] = useState<Song[]>([]);
  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<Album[]>([]);
  const [state, setState] = useState<"loading" | "ok" | "notfound">("loading");
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"posts" | "music">("posts");

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([getPublicProfile(id), listPostsByAuthor(id), listSongsByUploader(id), listAlbumsByOwner(id)])
      .then(([p, ps, ss, as]) => {
        setProfile(p);
        setPosts(ps);
        setSongs(ss);
        setAlbums(as);
        setState(p ? "ok" : "notfound");
        if (!ps.length && (ss.length || as.length)) setTab("music");
      })
      .catch(() => setState("notfound"));
  }, [id]);

  if (state === "loading") return <p className="py-16 text-center text-muted">Đang tải…</p>;
  if (!profile) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-ink-soft">Không tìm thấy tác giả.</p>;

  const tabCls = (on: boolean) => `rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm ${on ? "bg-ink text-paper" : "border border-line text-ink-soft hover:bg-soft"}`;

  return (
    <div>
      <section className="hero-glow flex flex-col items-center gap-6 rounded-[2rem] border border-line p-8 text-center md:flex-row md:text-left">
        <Avatar url={profile.avatar_url} name={profile.full_name} size={120} />
        <div className="flex-1">
          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Tác giả</p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">{profile.full_name ?? "Ẩn danh"}</h1>
          {profile.bio && <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl whitespace-pre-line text-ink-soft">{profile.bio}</p>}
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted">
            {posts.length} bài viết · {songs.length} bài hát · {albums.length} album · tham gia {formatDate(profile.created_at)}
          </p>
        </div>
        {user?.id === profile.id && <Link href="/ho-so" className="rounded-full border border-line px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink hover:bg-soft">✏️ Sửa hồ sơ</Link>}
      </section>

      <div className="my-8 flex gap-2">
        <button type="button" className={tabCls(tab === "posts")} onClick={() => setTab("posts")}>Bài viết ({posts.length})</button>
        <button type="button" className={tabCls(tab === "music")} onClick={() => setTab("music")}>Âm nhạc ({songs.length})</button>
      </div>

      {tab === "posts" ? (
        posts.length ? (
          <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">{posts.map((p) => <PostCard key={p.id} post={p} />)}</div>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-muted">Chưa có bài viết.</p>
        )
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-10">
          {albums.length > 0 && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-4">{albums.map((a) => <AlbumCard key={a.id} album={a} />)}</div>
          )}
          <div className="grid gap-3 lg:grid-cols-2">
            {songs.map((s) => (
              <SongCard key={s.id} song={s} active={current?.id === s.id} onPlay={() => playSong(s, songs, profile.full_name ?? undefined)} />
            ))}
          </div>
          {!songs.length && <p className="text-muted">Chưa có bài hát.</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import RequireAuth from "@/components/auth/RequireAuth";
import PostForm from "@/components/posts/PostForm";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { createPost } from "@/services/postService";
import { parseTags } from "@/utils/tags";

function Write() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[] | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    // /viet-bai?tag=thơ → điền sẵn hashtag
    const sp = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    setTags(parseTags(sp.get("tag") ?? ""));
  }, []);

  if (!tags) return null;
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-4">
      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Sáng tác</p>
      <h1 className="font-serif text-4xl font-semibold text-ink">Viết bài / Tải sách lên</h1>
      {!isAdmin && (
        <p className="rounded-xl bg-accent-soft p-3 text-sm text-ink-soft">Bài của bạn sẽ được quản trị viên duyệt trước khi hiển thị công khai.</p>
      )}
      <PostForm
        defaultTags={tags}
        submitLabel={isAdmin ? "Đăng bài" : "Gửi bài"}
        onCancel={() => router.back()}
        onSubmit={async (input) => {
          const post = await createPost(input, user!.id, isAdmin);
          router.push(isAdmin ? `/bai-viet/${post.slug}` : "/dashboard?sent=1");
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function WritePage() {
  return <RequireAuth><Write /></RequireAuth>;
}

=== FILE: src\components\auth\AuthProvider.tsx ===
"use client";

import { createContext, useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { User } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/auth";
import type { Profile } from "@/types/profile";

export interface AuthState {
  user: User | null;
  profile: Profile | null;
  loading: boolean;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  refreshProfile: () => Promise<void>;
}

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthState>({
  user: null,
  profile: null,
  loading: true,
  isAdmin: false,
  refreshProfile: async () => {},
});

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
  const [profileFor, setProfileFor] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data }) => {
      setUser(data.session?.user ?? null);
      setAuthLoading(false);
    });
    const { data: sub } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      setUser(session?.user ?? null);
      setAuthLoading(false);
    });
    return () => sub.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    let active = true;
    getProfile(user.id).then((p) => {
      if (!active) return;
      setProfile(p);
      setProfileFor(user.id);
    });
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [user]);

  const refreshProfile = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    setProfile(await getProfile(user.id));
    setProfileFor(user.id);
  }, [user]);

  const currentProfile = user && profileFor === user.id ? profile : null;
  const loading = authLoading || (!!user && profileFor !== user.id);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{ user, profile: currentProfile, loading, isAdmin: currentProfile?.role === "admin", refreshProfile }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\auth\RequireAuth.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";

export default function RequireAuth({ children, admin = false }: { children: React.ReactNode; admin?: boolean }) {
  const { user, isAdmin, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-muted">Đang tải…</p>;

  if (!user)
    return (
      <div className="py-16 text-center">
        <p className="mb-4 text-ink-soft">Vui lòng đăng nhập để tiếp tục.</p>
        <Link href="/login" className="rounded-full bg-ink px-5 py-2.5 text-paper">Đăng nhập</Link>
      </div>
    );

  if (admin && !isAdmin) return <p className="py-16 text-center text-ink-soft">Bạn không có quyền truy cập trang này.</p>;

  return <>{children}</>;
}

=== FILE: src\components\layout\ThemeToggle.tsx ===
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { MoonIcon, SunIcon } from "@/components/ui/Icons";

/** Mặc định là giao diện tối; lựa chọn của người dùng được ghi nhớ. */
export default function ThemeToggle() {
  const [dark, setDark] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    setDark(document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark"));
  }, []);

  function toggle() {
    const next = !dark;
    setDark(next);
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", next);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("theme", next ? "dark" : "light");
    } catch {}
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={toggle}
      aria-label={dark ? "Chuyển sang giao diện sáng" : "Chuyển sang giao diện tối"}
      title={dark ? "Giao diện sáng" : "Giao diện tối"}
      className="btn-icon border border-line"
    >
      {dark ? <SunIcon size={18} /> : <MoonIcon size={18} />}
    </button>
  );
}

import { TERMS, TERMS_MOTTO, TERMS_SITE, TERMS_TITLE } from "@/lib/terms";

/** Toàn văn Điều lệ (dùng ở trang /dieu-le và cửa sổ đồng ý) */
export default function TermsContent({ compact = false }: { compact?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className={compact ? "text-sm leading-relaxed" : "leading-relaxed"}>
      <header className="mb-6 text-center">
        <h2 className={`font-serif font-semibold text-ink ${compact ? "text-xl" : "text-3xl"}`}>{TERMS_TITLE}</h2>
        <p className={`mt-1 font-serif font-semibold text-accent ${compact ? "text-lg" : "text-2xl"}`}>{TERMS_SITE}</p>
        <p className="mt-2 italic text-muted">{TERMS_MOTTO}</p>
      </header>
      <div className="space-y-3 text-ink-soft">
        {TERMS.map((b, i) => {
          if (b.type === "h") return <h3 key={i} className="!mt-7 font-serif text-lg font-semibold text-ink">{b.text}</h3>;
          if (b.type === "ul")
            return (
              <ul key={i} className="list-disc space-y-1.5 pl-6 marker:text-accent">
                {b.items.map((it, j) => <li key={j}>{it}</li>)}
              </ul>
            );
          if (b.type === "quote")
            return <blockquote key={i} className="border-l-4 border-accent bg-accent-soft/40 px-4 py-3 font-serif italic text-ink">{b.text}</blockquote>;
          return <p key={i}>{b.text}</p>;
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\legal\TermsGate.tsx ===
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import TermsContent from "@/components/legal/TermsContent";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { acceptTerms, signOut } from "@/lib/auth";
import { TERMS_CHECKBOX_TEXT } from "@/lib/terms";

/**
 * Cửa sổ bắt buộc: tài khoản chưa đồng ý Điều lệ (đăng nhập Google lần đầu,
 * hoặc tài khoản cũ) phải tích đồng ý mới được tiếp tục sử dụng website.
 */
export default function TermsGate() {
  const { user, profile, loading, refreshProfile } = useAuth();
  const [agree, setAgree] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (loading || !user || !profile || profile.terms_accepted_at) return null;

  async function accept() {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await acceptTerms();
      await refreshProfile();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Không ghi nhận được. Vui lòng thử lại.");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 p-3 backdrop-blur-sm" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="terms-title">
      <div className="glass anim-in flex max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-3xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl !bg-[var(--surface)] shadow-2xl">
        <div className="border-b border-line px-6 py-4">
          <p id="terms-title" className="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-ink">Chào mừng bạn đến với Phật Tại Tâm 🙏</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">Vui lòng đọc và đồng ý Điều lệ để tiếp tục.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-y-auto px-6 py-5"><TermsContent compact /></div>
        <div className="space-y-4 border-t border-line bg-soft/50 px-6 py-4">
          <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 text-sm text-ink">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={agree} onChange={(e) => setAgree(e.target.checked)} className="mt-1 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 accent-[var(--accent)]" />
            <span>{TERMS_CHECKBOX_TEXT}</span>
          </label>
          {error && <p className="text-sm text-red-500">{error}</p>}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-3">
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => signOut()}>Không đồng ý — Đăng xuất</button>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" disabled={!agree || busy} onClick={accept}>
              {busy ? "Đang ghi nhận…" : "Đồng ý và tiếp tục"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\music\AddToPlaylist.tsx ===
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { addSongToPlaylist, createPlaylist, listMyPlaylists } from "@/services/playlistService";
import type { Playlist } from "@/types/social";

/** Nút "+" thêm bài hát vào playlist của tôi */
export default function AddToPlaylist({ songId }: { songId: string }) {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [lists, setLists] = useState<Playlist[]>([]);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function toggle() {
    if (!user) return router.push("/login");
    if (!open) setLists(await listMyPlaylists(user.id));
    setMsg(null);
    setOpen(!open);
  }

  async function add(p: Playlist) {
    await addSongToPlaylist(p.id, songId);
    setMsg(`Đã thêm vào “${p.title}”`);
    setTimeout(() => setOpen(false), 900);
  }

  async function createAndAdd() {
    if (!user) return;
    const title = prompt("Tên playlist mới:");
    if (!title?.trim()) return;
    const p = await createPlaylist(title, user.id);
    await add(p);
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={toggle}
        title="Thêm vào playlist"
        aria-label="Thêm vào playlist"
        className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-line text-ink-soft hover:bg-soft"
      >
        ＋
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 top-11 z-20 w-56 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-2 shadow-xl">
          {msg ? (
            <p className="p-2 text-sm text-green-700">{msg}</p>
          ) : (
            <>
              <p className="px-2 pb-1 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">Thêm vào playlist</p>
              {lists.map((p) => (
                <button key={p.id} type="button" onClick={() => add(p)} className="block w-full truncate rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-sm text-ink hover:bg-soft">
                  {p.title}
                </button>
              ))}
              <button type="button" onClick={createAndAdd} className="mt-1 block w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-sm text-accent hover:bg-soft">
                ＋ Tạo playlist mới
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\music\AlbumCard.tsx ===
import Link from "next/link";
import CoverImage from "@/components/ui/CoverImage";
import { albumCoverUrl, albumSongCount } from "@/services/albumService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";

/** Thẻ album: ảnh bìa (chưa có thì dùng logo trang web) + nhãn "Single" hoặc số bài hát */
export default function AlbumCard({ album }: { album: Album }) {
  const cover = albumCoverUrl(album);
  const count = albumSongCount(album);
  const badge = album.is_solo ? "Single" : count > 0 ? `${count} bài` : "Chưa có bài";

  return (
    <Link href={`/album/${album.id}`} className="group block">
      <div className="relative aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-accent-soft shadow-sm transition group-hover:-translate-y-1 group-hover:shadow-lg">
        {cover ? (
          <CoverImage src={cover} className="h-full w-full" />
        ) : (
          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
          <img src="/logo.png" alt="" loading="lazy" className="h-full w-full object-contain p-4" />
        )}
        <span className="absolute left-2 top-2 rounded-full bg-black/55 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium tracking-wide text-white backdrop-blur-sm">
          {badge}
        </span>
      </div>
      <p className="mt-3 line-clamp-1 font-serif font-semibold text-ink">{album.title}</p>
      <p className="line-clamp-1 text-sm text-muted">{album.owner?.full_name ?? ""}</p>
    </Link>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\music\AlbumForm.tsx ===
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import CoverImage from "@/components/ui/CoverImage";
import { albumCoverUrl } from "@/services/albumService";
import type { Album, AlbumInput } from "@/types/album";
import { IMAGE_MAX_INPUT_BYTES, compressImage, formatFileSize } from "@/utils/imageCompress";

export default function AlbumForm({
  album,
  submitLabel,
  onSubmit,
  onCancel,
}: {
  album?: Album | null;
  submitLabel: string;
  onSubmit: (input: AlbumInput) => Promise<void>;
  onCancel?: () => void;
}) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(album?.title ?? "");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(album?.description ?? "");
  const [newCover, setNewCover] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [removeCover, setRemoveCover] = useState(false);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(album ? albumCoverUrl(album) : null);
  const [info, setInfo] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [compressing, setCompressing] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Giải phóng địa chỉ xem trước tạm thời
  useEffect(() => () => { if (preview?.startsWith("blob:")) URL.revokeObjectURL(preview); }, [preview]);

  async function pick(f: File | null) {
    if (!f) return;
    if (!f.type.startsWith("image/")) return setError("Chỉ chấp nhận tệp ảnh (JPG, PNG, WebP).");
    if (f.size > IMAGE_MAX_INPUT_BYTES) return setError("Ảnh gốc quá lớn (tối đa 40 MB).");
    setError(null);
    setCompressing(true);
    try {
      const r = await compressImage(f);
      setNewCover(r.file);
      setRemoveCover(false);
      setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(r.file));
      setInfo(r.compressed ? `Đã tự nén ảnh: ${formatFileSize(r.originalSize)} → ${formatFileSize(r.file.size)}` : `Ảnh ${formatFileSize(f.size)} — giữ nguyên`);
    } finally {
      setCompressing(false);
    }
  }

  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onSubmit({ title, description, newCover, removeCover });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Không lưu được album");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const showCover = preview && !removeCover;

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-[14rem_1fr]">
      <div>
        <div className="aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-accent-soft">
          {showCover ? (
            <CoverImage src={preview} eager className="h-full w-full" />
          ) : (
            // Chưa có ảnh bìa: dùng logo trang web
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
            <img src="/logo.png" alt="Logo Phật Tại Tâm" className="h-full w-full object-contain p-5" />
          )}
        </div>
        {!showCover && <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-muted">Chưa có ảnh bìa — album sẽ hiện logo Phật Tại Tâm.</p>}
        <label className="mt-3 block text-sm text-ink-soft">
          <span className="mb-1 block font-medium text-ink">Ảnh bìa album</span>
          <input type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" onChange={(e) => pick(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)}
            className="block w-full text-sm file:mr-3 file:rounded-full file:border-0 file:bg-accent-soft file:px-4 file:py-2 file:text-accent" />
        </label>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted">Ảnh tỉ lệ nào cũng được — hiển thị vừa khung, không bị cắt. Ảnh lớn được tự nén xuống khoảng 1 MB.</p>
        {compressing && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted">Đang nén ảnh…</p>}
        {info && !compressing && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-green-500">{info}</p>}
        {album?.cover_path && !newCover && (
          <label className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-500">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={removeCover} onChange={(e) => setRemoveCover(e.target.checked)} className="accent-[var(--accent)]" /> Xoá ảnh bìa
          </label>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <input required value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="Tên album, ví dụ: Hơi Ấm Quê Mình" className="field font-serif text-xl" />
        <textarea value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} rows={6} placeholder="Giới thiệu album (không bắt buộc)" className="field" />
        {error && <p className="text-red-500">{error}</p>}
        <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row">
          {onCancel && <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="btn btn-secondary !py-3">Huỷ</button>}
          <button disabled={busy || compressing} className="btn btn-primary !px-7 !py-3 sm:order-first">{busy ? "Đang lưu…" : submitLabel}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\music\AudioPlayer.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { usePlayer } from "@/components/music/PlayerProvider";
import CoverImage from "@/components/ui/CoverImage";
import {
  CloseIcon, DownloadIcon, LinkIcon, LyricsIcon, MoreIcon, MuteIcon, NextIcon, PauseIcon,
  PlayIcon, PrevIcon, QueueIcon, RadioIcon, RepeatIcon, RepeatOneIcon, VolumeIcon,
} from "@/components/ui/Icons";
import { songCoverUrl, songDownloadUrl } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";

function fmt(s: number) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(s) || s < 0) return "0:00";
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
  return `${m}:${String(Math.floor(s % 60)).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/** Ảnh bìa vừa khung, không bị cắt: bài hát → album → logo trang web */
function Cover({ song, className, blur = false }: { song: Song; className: string; blur?: boolean }) {
  return <CoverImage src={songCoverUrl(song) ?? "/logo.png"} blur={blur} className={className} />;
}

/**
 * Trình phát cố định — kiểu kính mờ trong suốt (tham khảo Suno).
 * - Máy tính / tablet ngang: thanh phát đầy đủ ở cuối trang.
 * - Điện thoại: thanh mini; chạm vào để mở trình phát toàn màn hình.
 */
export default function AudioPlayer() {
  const {
    current, queue, index, radio, source, playing, currentTime, duration, volume, muted, repeat,
    togglePlay, next, prev, jumpTo, seek, setVolume, toggleMute, cycleRepeat, stop,
  } = usePlayer();

  const [lyricsOpen, setLyricsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [queueOpen, setQueueOpen] = useState(false);
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  /* Đóng menu khi bấm ra ngoài hoặc nhấn Esc */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menuOpen) return;
    const down = (e: PointerEvent) => { if (!menuRef.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setMenuOpen(false); };
    const key = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === "Escape") setMenuOpen(false); };
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", down);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", key);
    return () => { document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", down); document.removeEventListener("keydown", key); };
  }, [menuOpen]);

  /* Khoá cuộn trang khi mở trình phát toàn màn hình */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!expanded) return;
    const prevOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    const key = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === "Escape") setExpanded(false); };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", key);
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = prevOverflow; document.removeEventListener("keydown", key); };
  }, [expanded]);

  useEffect(() => { if (!current) setExpanded(false); }, [current]);

  if (!current) return null;

  const pct = duration > 0 ? Math.min(100, (currentTime / duration) * 100) : 0;
  const volPct = (muted ? 0 : volume) * 100;
  const RepeatBtnIcon = repeat === "one" ? RepeatOneIcon : RepeatIcon;
  const repeatLabel = repeat === "off" ? "Lặp lại: tắt" : repeat === "all" ? "Lặp lại danh sách" : "Lặp lại một bài";
  const subtitle = `${current.artist ?? "Phật Tại Tâm"}${source && !radio ? ` · ${source}` : ""}`;

  const showLyrics = () => { setLyricsOpen((v) => !v); setQueueOpen(false); setMenuOpen(false); };
  const showQueue = () => { setQueueOpen((v) => !v); setLyricsOpen(false); };
  const close = () => { stop(); setMenuOpen(false); setLyricsOpen(false); setQueueOpen(false); setExpanded(false); };

  const radioBadge = radio && (
    <span className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-accent px-1.5 py-px text-[10px] font-bold text-[var(--on-accent)]">
      <RadioIcon size={10} /> RADIO
    </span>
  );

  const progress = (big = false) => (
    <div className={`flex w-full items-center gap-2 tabular-nums text-muted ${big ? "text-xs" : "text-[11px]"}`}>
      <span className={big ? "w-10" : "w-9 text-right"}>{fmt(currentTime)}</span>
      <input
        type="range"
        className="range"
        min={0}
        max={duration || 0}
        step={0.1}
        value={Math.min(currentTime, duration || 0)}
        onChange={(e) => seek(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
        style={{ "--pct": `${pct}%` } as React.CSSProperties}
        aria-label="Tua bài hát"
      />
      <span className={big ? "w-10 text-right" : "w-9"}>{fmt(duration)}</span>
    </div>
  );

  const menu = (placement: string) => (
    <div className="relative" ref={menuRef}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={`btn-icon ${menuOpen ? "is-on" : ""}`}
        onClick={() => setMenuOpen((v) => !v)}
        aria-label="Thêm tuỳ chọn"
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={menuOpen}
      >
        <MoreIcon size={20} />
      </button>
      {menuOpen && (
        <div role="menu" className={`glass anim-in absolute right-0 z-50 w-64 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl !bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--surface)_92%,transparent)] p-1.5 shadow-2xl ${placement}`}>
          <button role="menuitem" type="button" className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-left text-sm text-ink transition hover-soft" onClick={showLyrics}>
            <LyricsIcon size={18} /> {lyricsOpen ? "Ẩn lời bài hát" : "Hiển thị lời bài hát"}
          </button>
          <Link role="menuitem" href={`/bai-hat/${current.id}`} onClick={() => { setMenuOpen(false); setExpanded(false); }}
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-ink transition hover-soft">
            <LinkIcon size={18} /> Mở trang bài hát
          </Link>
          <a role="menuitem" href={songDownloadUrl(current)} onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-ink transition hover-soft">
            <DownloadIcon size={18} /> Tải về
          </a>
          <button role="menuitem" type="button" className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-left text-sm text-ink transition hover-soft md:hidden"
            onClick={() => { toggleMute(); setMenuOpen(false); }}>
            {muted ? <VolumeIcon size={18} /> : <MuteIcon size={18} />} {muted ? "Bật tiếng" : "Tắt tiếng"}
          </button>
          <div className="my-1 h-px bg-line" />
          <button role="menuitem" type="button" className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-left text-sm text-[#ef6a6a] transition hover-soft" onClick={close}>
            <CloseIcon size={18} /> Đóng trình phát
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );

  const lyricsBody = current.lyrics ? (
    <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-center font-serif text-lg leading-loose text-ink">{current.lyrics}</p>
  ) : (
    <p className="py-6 text-center text-muted">Bài hát này chưa có lời.</p>
  );

  const queueList = (
    <ul>
      {queue.map((s, i) => (
        <li key={`${s.id}-${i}`}>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => jumpTo(i)}
            className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-left transition hover-soft ${i === index ? "row-active" : ""}`}>
            <span className="w-6 text-center text-xs text-muted">
              {i === index ? <span className="text-accent">{playing ? "▶" : "❚❚"}</span> : i + 1}
            </span>
            <Cover song={s} className="h-9 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-lg" />
            <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <span className={`block truncate text-sm ${i === index ? "font-semibold text-accent" : "text-ink"}`}>{s.title}</span>
              <span className="block truncate text-xs text-muted">{s.artist ?? "Phật Tại Tâm"}</span>
            </span>
          </button>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );

  return (
    <>
      {/* ================= ĐIỆN THOẠI: TRÌNH PHÁT TOÀN MÀN HÌNH ================= */}
      {expanded && (
        <div className="anim-sheet fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col overflow-hidden bg-paper md:hidden" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Trình phát nhạc">
          {/* Nền mờ lấy từ ảnh bìa */}
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img src={songCoverUrl(current) ?? "/logo.png"} alt="" aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 h-full w-full scale-125 object-cover opacity-30 blur-3xl" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-paper/40 via-paper/70 to-paper" aria-hidden />

          <div className="safe-top safe-x relative flex items-center justify-between">
            <button type="button" className="btn-icon" onClick={() => setExpanded(false)} aria-label="Thu nhỏ trình phát">
              <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" aria-hidden><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6" /></svg>
            </button>
            <p className="min-w-0 truncate px-2 text-center text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">
              {radio ? "Radio Phật Tại Tâm" : source ?? "Đang phát"}
            </p>
            {menu("top-12")}
          </div>

          <div className="safe-x relative flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
            {lyricsOpen || queueOpen ? (
              <div className="glass my-4 min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto rounded-3xl p-4">
                <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{lyricsOpen ? "Lời bài hát" : `Danh sách phát (${queue.length})`}</p>
                  <button type="button" className="btn-icon" onClick={() => { setLyricsOpen(false); setQueueOpen(false); }} aria-label="Đóng"><CloseIcon size={18} /></button>
                </div>
                {lyricsOpen ? lyricsBody : queueList}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center py-6">
                <Cover song={current} blur className={`aspect-square w-full max-w-[22rem] rounded-3xl shadow-2xl transition duration-500 ${playing ? "scale-100" : "scale-[0.92] opacity-90"}`} />
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="space-y-4 pb-2">
              <div className="flex items-end gap-3">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className="truncate font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-ink">{current.title}</p>
                  <p className="flex items-center gap-2 truncate text-sm text-muted">{radioBadge}<span className="truncate">{subtitle}</span></p>
                </div>
                <button type="button" className={`btn-icon ${lyricsOpen ? "is-on" : ""}`} onClick={showLyrics} aria-label="Hiển thị lời bài hát" aria-pressed={lyricsOpen}>
                  <LyricsIcon size={20} />
                </button>
              </div>

              {progress(true)}

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <button type="button" className={`btn-icon !h-12 !w-12 ${repeat !== "off" ? "is-on" : ""}`} onClick={cycleRepeat} aria-label={repeatLabel} aria-pressed={repeat !== "off"}>
                  <RepeatBtnIcon size={22} />
                </button>
                <button type="button" className="btn-icon !h-14 !w-14" onClick={prev} aria-label="Bài trước"><PrevIcon size={30} /></button>
                <button type="button" onClick={togglePlay} aria-label={playing ? "Tạm dừng" : "Phát"} className="btn-primary flex h-[4.5rem] w-[4.5rem] items-center justify-center rounded-full">
                  {playing ? <PauseIcon size={34} /> : <PlayIcon size={34} />}
                </button>
                <button type="button" className="btn-icon !h-14 !w-14" onClick={next} aria-label="Bài tiếp"><NextIcon size={30} /></button>
                <button type="button" className={`btn-icon !h-12 !w-12 ${queueOpen ? "is-on" : ""}`} onClick={showQueue} aria-label="Danh sách phát" aria-pressed={queueOpen}>
                  <QueueIcon size={22} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="safe-bottom" />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="safe-bottom fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 px-2 sm:px-4 md:pb-4">
        {/* ===== Máy tính: Lời bài hát / Hàng đợi ===== */}
        {lyricsOpen && !expanded && (
          <div className="glass anim-in mx-auto mb-2 hidden max-h-[55vh] max-w-5xl overflow-y-auto rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl md:block">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted">Lời bài hát</p>
                <p className="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-ink">{current.title}</p>
              </div>
              <button type="button" className="btn-icon" onClick={() => setLyricsOpen(false)} aria-label="Đóng lời bài hát"><CloseIcon size={18} /></button>
            </div>
            {lyricsBody}
          </div>
        )}
        {queueOpen && !expanded && (
          <div className="glass anim-in mx-auto mb-2 hidden max-h-[50vh] max-w-5xl overflow-y-auto rounded-3xl p-3 shadow-2xl md:block">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">
                {radio ? "Radio — sắp phát" : "Danh sách phát"} <span className="font-normal text-muted">({queue.length} bài)</span>
              </p>
              <button type="button" className="btn-icon" onClick={() => setQueueOpen(false)} aria-label="Đóng danh sách"><CloseIcon size={18} /></button>
            </div>
            {queueList}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ================= ĐIỆN THOẠI: THANH MINI ================= */}
        <div className="glass relative mx-auto overflow-hidden rounded-2xl shadow-2xl md:hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-0.5 bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--ink)_12%,transparent)]" aria-hidden>
            <div className="h-full bg-accent transition-[width] duration-300" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-2 pl-2 pr-1">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setExpanded(true)} className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-3 text-left" aria-label="Mở trình phát toàn màn hình">
              <Cover song={current} className="h-11 w-11 shrink-0 rounded-xl" />
              <span className="min-w-0">
                <span className="block truncate text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{current.title}</span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate text-xs text-muted">{radioBadge}<span className="truncate">{subtitle}</span></span>
              </span>
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={togglePlay} aria-label={playing ? "Tạm dừng" : "Phát"} className="btn-icon !h-11 !w-11 text-ink">
              {playing ? <PauseIcon size={26} /> : <PlayIcon size={26} />}
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={next} aria-label="Bài tiếp" className="btn-icon !h-11 !w-11"><NextIcon size={22} /></button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ================= MÁY TÍNH: THANH PHÁT ĐẦY ĐỦ ================= */}
        <div className="glass mx-auto hidden max-w-5xl rounded-3xl px-5 pb-2 pt-2.5 shadow-2xl md:block">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_minmax(0,1.5fr)_1fr] items-center gap-x-3">
            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
              <Cover song={current} className={`h-14 w-14 shrink-0 rounded-xl ${playing ? "" : "opacity-80"}`} />
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <Link href={`/bai-hat/${current.id}`} className="block truncate text-sm font-semibold text-ink hover:underline">{current.title}</Link>
                <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate text-xs text-muted">{radioBadge}<span className="truncate">{subtitle}</span></p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button type="button" className={`btn-icon ${repeat !== "off" ? "is-on" : ""}`} onClick={cycleRepeat} aria-label={repeatLabel} title={repeatLabel} aria-pressed={repeat !== "off"}>
                  <RepeatBtnIcon size={18} />
                </button>
                <button type="button" className="btn-icon" onClick={prev} aria-label="Bài trước"><PrevIcon size={20} /></button>
                <button type="button" onClick={togglePlay} aria-label={playing ? "Tạm dừng" : "Phát"} className="btn-primary flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full">
                  {playing ? <PauseIcon size={22} /> : <PlayIcon size={22} />}
                </button>
                <button type="button" className="btn-icon" onClick={next} aria-label="Bài tiếp"><NextIcon size={20} /></button>
                <button type="button" className={`btn-icon ${queueOpen ? "is-on" : ""}`} onClick={showQueue} aria-label="Danh sách phát" title="Danh sách phát" aria-pressed={queueOpen}>
                  <QueueIcon size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>
              {progress()}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
              <button type="button" className="btn-icon" onClick={toggleMute} aria-label={muted ? "Bật tiếng" : "Tắt tiếng"}>
                {muted || volume === 0 ? <MuteIcon size={18} /> : <VolumeIcon size={18} />}
              </button>
              <input
                type="range"
                className="range !w-24"
                min={0}
                max={1}
                step={0.01}
                value={muted ? 0 : volume}
                onChange={(e) => setVolume(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                style={{ "--pct": `${volPct}%` } as React.CSSProperties}
                aria-label="Âm lượng"
              />
              {!expanded && menu("bottom-12")}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\music\PlayerProvider.tsx ===
"use client";

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { countSongPlay } from "@/services/socialService";
import { songAudioUrl, songCoverUrl } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";

export type RepeatMode = "off" | "all" | "one";

interface PlayerState {
  current: Song | null;
  queue: Song[];
  index: number;
  radio: boolean;
  /** Tên nguồn đang phát: "Radio", tên album, tên playlist… */
  source: string | null;
  playing: boolean;
  currentTime: number;
  duration: number;
  volume: number;
  muted: boolean;
  repeat: RepeatMode;
  playSong: (song: Song, list?: Song[], source?: string) => void;
  playList: (list: Song[], source?: string) => void;
  startRadio: (list: Song[], source?: string, shuffle?: boolean) => void;
  togglePlay: () => void;
  next: () => void;
  prev: () => void;
  jumpTo: (index: number) => void;
  seek: (seconds: number) => void;
  setVolume: (v: number) => void;
  toggleMute: () => void;
  cycleRepeat: () => void;
  stop: () => void;
}

const PlayerContext = createContext<PlayerState | null>(null);

function shuffle<T>(arr: T[]): T[] {
  const a = [...arr];
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
}

/** Đoán định dạng ảnh theo đuôi tệp (màn hình khoá cần biết loại ảnh) */
function imageType(url: string): string {
  const ext = url.split("?")[0].split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase();
  if (ext === "jpg" || ext === "jpeg") return "image/jpeg";
  if (ext === "webp") return "image/webp";
  if (ext === "gif") return "image/gif";
  return "image/png";
}

/**
 * Ảnh cho màn hình khoá / trung tâm điều khiển (iPhone, iPad, Android, máy tính):
 * ảnh bìa bài hát → bìa album → logo Phật Tại Tâm. Phải là địa chỉ tuyệt đối.
 */
function artworkFor(song: Song): MediaImage[] {
  const origin = window.location.origin;
  const cover = songCoverUrl(song);
  if (cover) {
    const abs = cover.startsWith("http") ? cover : `${origin}${cover}`;
    const type = imageType(abs);
    return ["96x96", "192x192", "256x256", "384x384", "512x512"].map((sizes) => ({ src: abs, sizes, type }));
  }
  return [
    { src: `${origin}/logo-sm.png`, sizes: "128x128", type: "image/png" },
    { src: `${origin}/logo.png`, sizes: "512x512", type: "image/png" },
  ];
}

export function PlayerProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const audioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement>(null);
  const [queue, setQueue] = useState<Song[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [radio, setRadio] = useState(false);
  const [radioShuffle, setRadioShuffle] = useState(true);
  const [source, setSource] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [token, setToken] = useState(0);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0);
  const [volume, setVolumeState] = useState(0.9);
  const [muted, setMuted] = useState(false);
  const [repeat, setRepeat] = useState<RepeatMode>("off");

  const bump = () => setToken((t) => t + 1);
  const current = queue[index] ?? null;

  /* Nhớ âm lượng & chế độ lặp */
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const v = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem("player:volume") ?? "");
      if (!Number.isNaN(v)) setVolumeState(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v)));
      const r = localStorage.getItem("player:repeat");
      if (r === "all" || r === "one" || r === "off") setRepeat(r);
    } catch {}
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (a) {
      a.volume = volume;
      a.muted = muted;
    }
  }, [volume, muted]);

  /* Bắt đầu phát khi đổi bài */
  useEffect(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (!a || !current) return;
    a.src = songAudioUrl(current);
    a.currentTime = 0;
    setCurrentTime(0);
    setDuration(0);
    a.play().catch(() => setPlaying(false));
    countSongPlay(current.id).catch(() => {});
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [current?.id, token]);

  const playSong = useCallback((song: Song, list?: Song[], src?: string) => {
    const q = list && list.length ? list : [song];
    setQueue(q);
    setIndex(Math.max(0, q.findIndex((s) => s.id === song.id)));
    setRadio(false);
    setSource(src ?? null);
    bump();
  }, []);

  const playList = useCallback((list: Song[], src?: string) => {
    if (!list.length) return;
    setQueue(list);
    setIndex(0);
    setRadio(false);
    setSource(src ?? null);
    bump();
  }, []);

  /** Radio: phát danh sách (ngẫu nhiên hoặc theo thứ tự), hết vòng tự phát lại từ đầu */
  const startRadio = useCallback((list: Song[], src?: string, shuffleOn = true) => {
    if (!list.length) return;
    setQueue(shuffleOn ? shuffle(list) : list);
    setIndex(0);
    setRadio(true);
    setRadioShuffle(shuffleOn);
    setSource(src ?? "Radio Phật Tại Tâm");
    bump();
  }, []);

  const next = useCallback(() => {
    if (index + 1 < queue.length) {
      setIndex(index + 1);
      bump();
    } else if (radio) {
      setQueue(radioShuffle ? shuffle(queue) : queue);
      setIndex(0);
      bump();
    } else if (repeat === "all" && queue.length) {
      setIndex(0);
      bump();
    }
  }, [index, queue, radio, radioShuffle, repeat]);

  const prev = useCallback(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (a && a.currentTime > 3) {
      a.currentTime = 0;
      return;
    }
    if (index > 0) {
      setIndex(index - 1);
      bump();
    } else if (a) {
      a.currentTime = 0;
    }
  }, [index]);

  const jumpTo = useCallback((i: number) => {
    setIndex(i);
    bump();
  }, []);

  const togglePlay = useCallback(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (!a || !current) return;
    if (a.paused) a.play().catch(() => {});
    else a.pause();
  }, [current]);

  const seek = useCallback((s: number) => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (a && Number.isFinite(s)) {
      a.currentTime = Math.max(0, Math.min(s, a.duration || s));
      setCurrentTime(a.currentTime);
    }
  }, []);

  const setVolume = useCallback((v: number) => {
    const x = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v));
    setVolumeState(x);
    if (x > 0) setMuted(false);
    try { localStorage.setItem("player:volume", String(x)); } catch {}
  }, []);

  const toggleMute = useCallback(() => setMuted((m) => !m), []);

  const cycleRepeat = useCallback(() => {
    setRepeat((r) => {
      const n: RepeatMode = r === "off" ? "all" : r === "all" ? "one" : "off";
      try { localStorage.setItem("player:repeat", n); } catch {}
      return n;
    });
  }, []);

  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    audioRef.current?.pause();
    setQueue([]);
    setIndex(0);
    setRadio(false);
    setSource(null);
    setPlaying(false);
    if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && "mediaSession" in navigator) {
      navigator.mediaSession.metadata = null;
      navigator.mediaSession.playbackState = "none";
    }
  }, []);

  /* Hết bài: lặp một bài / bài kế tiếp / lặp lại danh sách / dừng */
  const onEnded = useCallback(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (repeat === "one" && a) {
      a.currentTime = 0;
      a.play().catch(() => {});
      return;
    }
    if (index + 1 < queue.length || radio || (repeat === "all" && queue.length)) next();
    else setPlaying(false);
  }, [repeat, index, queue.length, radio, next]);

  /* ===== Màn hình khoá / tai nghe / trung tâm điều khiển ===== */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!current || typeof navigator === "undefined" || !("mediaSession" in navigator)) return;
    navigator.mediaSession.metadata = new MediaMetadata({
      title: current.title,
      artist: current.artist ?? "Phật Tại Tâm",
      album: current.album_info?.title ?? source ?? "Phật Tại Tâm",
      artwork: artworkFor(current),
    });
  }, [current, source]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof navigator === "undefined" || !("mediaSession" in navigator)) return;
    const ms = navigator.mediaSession;
    const set = (action: MediaSessionAction, handler: MediaSessionActionHandler | null) => {
      try { ms.setActionHandler(action, handler); } catch {}
    };
    set("play", () => audioRef.current?.play().catch(() => {}));
    set("pause", () => audioRef.current?.pause());
    set("nexttrack", next);
    set("previoustrack", prev);
    set("seekto", (d) => { if (typeof d.seekTime === "number") seek(d.seekTime); });
    set("seekbackward", (d) => seek((audioRef.current?.currentTime ?? 0) - (d.seekOffset || 10)));
    set("seekforward", (d) => seek((audioRef.current?.currentTime ?? 0) + (d.seekOffset || 10)));
  }, [next, prev, seek]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof navigator === "undefined" || !("mediaSession" in navigator)) return;
    navigator.mediaSession.playbackState = current ? (playing ? "playing" : "paused") : "none";
  }, [playing, current]);

  /* Thanh thời gian trên màn hình khoá */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof navigator === "undefined" || !("mediaSession" in navigator) || !duration || !Number.isFinite(duration)) return;
    try {
      navigator.mediaSession.setPositionState({
        duration,
        playbackRate: audioRef.current?.playbackRate || 1,
        position: Math.min(currentTime, duration),
      });
    } catch {}
    // Cập nhật mỗi khi đổi bài, phát/dừng hoặc tua — không cần mỗi giây
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [duration, playing, current?.id]);

  const seekAndSync = useCallback((s: number) => {
    seek(s);
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (a && "mediaSession" in navigator && a.duration) {
      try { navigator.mediaSession.setPositionState({ duration: a.duration, playbackRate: a.playbackRate || 1, position: a.currentTime }); } catch {}
    }
  }, [seek]);

  return (
    <PlayerContext.Provider
      value={{
        current, queue, index, radio, source, playing, currentTime, duration, volume, muted, repeat,
        playSong, playList, startRadio, togglePlay, next, prev, jumpTo, seek: seekAndSync, setVolume, toggleMute, cycleRepeat, stop,
      }}
    >
      {children}
      <audio
        ref={audioRef}
        preload="auto"
        playsInline
        onPlay={() => setPlaying(true)}
        onPause={() => setPlaying(false)}
        onTimeUpdate={(e) => setCurrentTime(e.currentTarget.currentTime)}
        onLoadedMetadata={(e) => setDuration(e.currentTarget.duration)}
        onDurationChange={(e) => setDuration(e.currentTarget.duration)}
        onEnded={onEnded}
        onError={() => setPlaying(false)}
      />
    </PlayerContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function usePlayer() {
  const ctx = useContext(PlayerContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("usePlayer phải dùng bên trong PlayerProvider");
  return ctx;
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import AddToPlaylist from "@/components/music/AddToPlaylist";
import CoverImage from "@/components/ui/CoverImage";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { songCoverUrl, songDownloadUrl } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Song } from "@/types/song";
import { formatCount } from "@/utils/formatDate";

export default function SongCard({
  song,
  active,
  onPlay,
  trackNo,
  extra,
}: {
  song: Song;
  active?: boolean;
  onPlay: () => void;
  trackNo?: number;
  extra?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const { user, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const cover = songCoverUrl(song);
  const canEdit = !!user && (user.id === song.uploader_id || isAdmin);
  const iconBtn = "flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-line text-ink-soft hover:bg-soft";

  return (
    <div
      className={`flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border bg-surface p-2.5 transition sm:p-3 ${
        active ? "border-accent ring-1 ring-accent" : "border-line hover:shadow-md"
      }`}
    >
      {trackNo !== undefined && <span className="w-5 text-center text-sm text-muted sm:w-6">{trackNo}</span>}
      <button type="button" onClick={onPlay} className="group relative shrink-0" aria-label={`Phát ${song.title}`}>
        <CoverImage src={cover ?? "/logo-sm.png"} blur={false} className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl sm:h-14 sm:w-14" />
        <span className={`absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-black/35 text-xl text-white transition ${active ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"}`}>
          {active ? "♪" : "▶"}
        </span>
      </button>
      {/* Điện thoại: chạm vào tên bài cũng phát nhạc */}
      <button type="button" onClick={onPlay} className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-left sm:hidden">
        <span className={`block truncate font-medium ${active ? "text-accent" : "text-ink"}`}>{song.title}</span>
        <span className="block truncate text-xs text-muted">{song.artist ?? "Không rõ"}{song.album_info ? ` · ${song.album_info.title}` : ""}</span>
        <span className="block text-[11px] text-muted">▶ {formatCount(song.play_count)}{song.lyrics ? " · có lời" : ""}</span>
      </button>
      <div className="hidden min-w-0 flex-1 sm:block">
        <Link href={`/bai-hat/${song.id}`} className={`block truncate font-medium hover:underline ${active ? "text-accent" : "text-ink"}`}>{song.title}</Link>
        <p className="truncate text-sm text-muted">
          {song.artist ?? "Không rõ"}
          {song.album_info && (
            <>
              {" · "}
              <Link href={`/album/${song.album_info.id}`} className="hover:underline">{song.album_info.title}</Link>
            </>
          )}
        </p>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">▶ {formatCount(song.play_count)} lượt nghe{song.lyrics ? " · có lời" : ""}</p>
      </div>
      <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
        {extra}
        <AddToPlaylist songId={song.id} />
        {canEdit && <Link href={`/sua-nhac/${song.id}`} title="Sửa" className={`${iconBtn} hidden sm:flex`}>✏️</Link>}
        <a href={songDownloadUrl(song)} title="Tải về" className={`${iconBtn} hidden sm:flex`}>⬇</a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\music\SongForm.tsx ===
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { MAX_UPLOAD_MB } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { listAlbums, listAlbumsByOwner } from "@/services/albumService";
import { songAudioUrl, songCoverUrl } from "@/services/songService";
import type { Album } from "@/types/album";
import type { Song, SongInput } from "@/types/song";

/** Form dùng chung cho Tải nhạc lên và Sửa bài hát */
export default function SongForm({
  song,
  submitLabel,
  onSubmit,
  onCancel,
}: {
  song?: Song | null;
  submitLabel: string;
  onSubmit: (input: SongInput) => Promise<void>;
  onCancel?: () => void;
}) {
  const { user, profile, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<Album[]>([]);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(song?.title ?? "");
  const [artist, setArtist] = useState(song?.artist ?? profile?.full_name ?? "");
  // Bài Single (album solo) = "không chọn album"
  const [albumId, setAlbumId] = useState<string>(song?.album_info?.is_solo ? "" : (song?.album_id ?? ""));
  const [trackNo, setTrackNo] = useState<string>(song?.track_no ? String(song.track_no) : "");
  const [lyrics, setLyrics] = useState(song?.lyrics ?? "");
  const [newAudio, setNewAudio] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [newCover, setNewCover] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [removeCover, setRemoveCover] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    // Danh sách chỉ gồm album thật (không gồm các Single)
    (isAdmin ? listAlbums() : listAlbumsByOwner(user.id)).then(setAlbums).catch(() => {});
  }, [user, isAdmin]);

  const limit = MAX_UPLOAD_MB * 1024 * 1024;

  function pickAudio(f: File | null) {
    setNewAudio(f);
    if (f && !title) setTitle(f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ""));
  }

  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!song && !newAudio) return setError("Hãy chọn tệp nhạc.");
    if (newAudio && newAudio.size > limit) return setError(`Tệp nhạc vượt quá ${MAX_UPLOAD_MB} MB.`);
    if (newCover && newCover.size > 40 * 1024 * 1024) return setError("Ảnh bìa gốc quá lớn (tối đa 40 MB).");
    const n = parseInt(trackNo, 10);
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onSubmit({
        title, artist, lyrics,
        albumId: albumId || null,
        trackNo: Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : null,
        newAudio, newCover, removeCover,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Không lưu được");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const drop = "block rounded-xl border border-dashed border-line bg-surface p-4 text-sm text-ink-soft";
  const cover = song ? songCoverUrl(song) : null;

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
      {song && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-4">
          {cover && !removeCover ? (
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
            <img src={cover} alt="" className="h-20 w-20 rounded-xl object-contain" />
          ) : (
            <div className="flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-accent-soft text-3xl">🎵</div>
          )}
          <audio controls src={songAudioUrl(song)} className="h-10 min-w-0 flex-1" />
        </div>
      )}

      <label className={drop}>
        <span className="mb-2 block font-medium text-ink">
          🎵 {song ? "Thay tệp nhạc (không bắt buộc)" : "Tệp nhạc"} — MP3, M4A, WAV, OGG, tối đa {MAX_UPLOAD_MB} MB
        </span>
        <input required={!song} type="file" accept="audio/*" onChange={(e) => pickAudio(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} className="block w-full text-sm" />
      </label>

      <input required value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="Tên bài hát" className="field" />
      <input value={artist} onChange={(e) => setArtist(e.target.value)} placeholder="Ca sĩ / tác giả" className="field" />

      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_8rem]">
        <select value={albumId} onChange={(e) => setAlbumId(e.target.value)} className="field">
          <option value="">— Không chọn album (Single) —</option>
          {albums.map((a) => (
            <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>{a.title}{isAdmin && a.owner?.full_name ? ` (${a.owner.full_name})` : ""}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <input type="number" min={1} value={trackNo} onChange={(e) => setTrackNo(e.target.value)} placeholder="Số thứ tự" className="field" />
      </div>
      <p className="-mt-2 text-xs text-muted">
        Không chọn album: bài hát tự thành một <b>Single</b> (album solo) và vẫn có trang album riêng.{" "}
        Chưa có album? <Link href="/album/moi" target="_blank" className="text-accent hover:underline">Tạo album mới</Link> rồi tải lại trang này.
      </p>

      <label className="block">
        <span className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-ink">📝 Lời bài hát</span>
        <textarea value={lyrics} onChange={(e) => setLyrics(e.target.value)} rows={12}
          placeholder={"[Verse 1]\n…\n\n[Chorus]\n…"} className="field font-serif leading-relaxed" />
      </label>

      <div className={drop}>
        <span className="mb-2 block font-medium text-ink">🖼 Ảnh bìa riêng (không bắt buộc — nếu trống sẽ dùng bìa album)</span>
        {song?.cover_path && !newCover && (
          <label className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={removeCover} onChange={(e) => setRemoveCover(e.target.checked)} className="accent-[var(--accent)]" />
            <span className="text-red-500">Xoá ảnh bìa hiện tại</span>
          </label>
        )}
        <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={(e) => { setNewCover(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null); setRemoveCover(false); }} className="block w-full text-sm" />
        <span className="mt-1 block text-[11px] text-muted">Ảnh lớn được tự nén xuống khoảng 1 MB khi tải lên.</span>
      </div>

      {error && <p className="text-red-500">{error}</p>}
      <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row">
        {onCancel && <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="btn btn-secondary !py-3">Huỷ</button>}
        <button disabled={busy} className="btn btn-primary !px-7 !py-3 sm:order-first">{busy ? "Đang lưu…" : submitLabel}</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

=== FILE: src\components\posts\BookCard.tsx ===
import Link from "next/link";
import CoverImage from "@/components/ui/CoverImage";
import type { Post } from "@/types/post";
import { authorLabel } from "@/types/post";
import { formatCount } from "@/utils/formatDate";

const PALETTES: [string, string][] = [
  ["#7a4a14", "#d9a441"],
  ["#6b3a2e", "#d98c6b"],
  ["#2f5a4f", "#8cc3a8"],
  ["#3b3f6b", "#9aa3e0"],
  ["#6b4a12", "#e0b75a"],
  ["#5a2f4a", "#d98cb8"],
];

export function coverColors(seed: string): [string, string] {
  let h = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < seed.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + seed.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  return PALETTES[h % PALETTES.length];
}

/** Thẻ dạng "bìa sách": có ảnh bìa thì hiện nguyên ảnh (vừa khung, không cắt), không có thì bìa màu kèm tên sách */
export default function BookCard({ post }: { post: Post }) {
  const [c1, c2] = coverColors(post.slug);
  const label = post.tags[0] ? `#${post.tags[0]}` : post.file_path ? "Sách" : "Bài viết";
  return (
    <Link href={`/bai-viet/${post.slug}`} className="group block">
      <div
        className="book-cover relative flex aspect-[3/4] flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-l-sm rounded-r-xl p-3 text-white transition duration-300 group-hover:-translate-y-1.5 group-hover:shadow-2xl sm:p-4"
        style={{ "--c1": c1, "--c2": c2 } as React.CSSProperties}
      >
        {post.cover_image ? (
          <>
            <CoverImage src={post.cover_image} className="absolute inset-0 !bg-transparent" />
            <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-1.5 bg-black/25" aria-hidden />
            <span className="relative self-start rounded-full bg-black/55 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] tracking-wide backdrop-blur-sm">{label}</span>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <span className="relative self-start rounded-full bg-black/30 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] tracking-wide">{label}</span>
            <span className="relative line-clamp-4 font-serif text-base font-semibold leading-snug drop-shadow sm:text-lg">{post.title}</span>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2.5 sm:mt-3">
        <p className="line-clamp-2 text-sm font-medium text-ink sm:text-base">{post.title}</p>
        <p className="line-clamp-1 text-xs text-muted sm:text-sm">Tác giả: {authorLabel(post)}</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-accent">
          {post.file_path ? "📎 Có tệp" : "Đọc online"} · 👁 {formatCount(post.view_count)}
        </p>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}
