/** true nếu đã được sửa sau khi đăng (chênh hơn 1 phút) */
export function wasEdited(createdAt: string, updatedAt?: string | null): boolean {
  if (!updatedAt) return false;
  return new Date(updatedAt).getTime() - new Date(createdAt).getTime() > 60_000;
}

export function formatCount(n: number | null | undefined): string {
  const v = n ?? 0;
  if (v >= 1_000_000) return `${(v / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, "")}tr`;
  if (v >= 1_000) return `${(v / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, "")}k`;
  return String(v);
}

=== FILE: src\utils\html.ts ===
/** Bài cũ là văn bản thuần; bài mới (trình soạn thảo) là HTML */
export function isHtml(content: string): boolean {
  return /^\s*<(p|h2|h3|ul|ol|blockquote|div|img|figure|b|strong|i|em|br|hr)[\s>/]/i.test(content);
}

/** Bỏ thẻ HTML để làm đoạn trích / mô tả SEO (chạy được cả server và client) */
export function stripHtml(content: string): string {
  return content
    .replace(/<(br|\/p|\/h2|\/h3|\/li|\/blockquote)\s*\/?>/gi, "\n")
    .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, "")
    .replace(/&nbsp;/g, " ")
    .replace(/&amp;/g, "&")
    .replace(/&lt;/g, "<")
    .replace(/&gt;/g, ">")
    .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
    .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
    .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
    .trim();
}

export function plainExcerpt(content: string, max = 200): string {
  const text = stripHtml(content).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return text.length > max ? `${text.slice(0, max).trimEnd()}…` : text;
}

/** Ảnh đầu tiên trong bài (dùng làm ảnh chia sẻ) */
export function firstImage(content: string): string | null {
  const m = content.match(/<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']/i);
  return m ? m[1] : null;
}

const ALLOWED: Record<string, string[]> = {
  P: [], BR: [], B: [], STRONG: [], I: [], EM: [], U: [], S: [], H2: [], H3: [],
  BLOCKQUOTE: [], UL: [], OL: [], LI: [], HR: [], DIV: [], SPAN: [], FIGURE: [], FIGCAPTION: [],
  A: ["href"],
  IMG: ["src", "alt"],
};

function safeUrl(url: string, forImg: boolean): boolean {
  const u = url.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (forImg) return u.startsWith("https://") || u.startsWith("http://");
  return u.startsWith("https://") || u.startsWith("http://") || u.startsWith("mailto:") || u.startsWith("/");
}

/** Lọc HTML: chỉ giữ thẻ/thuộc tính an toàn (chống mã độc XSS). Chỉ chạy ở trình duyệt. */
export function sanitizeHtml(dirty: string): string {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return "";
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<div>${dirty}</div>`, "text/html");
  const root = doc.body.firstElementChild as HTMLElement;

  const walk = (node: Element) => {
    Array.from(node.children).forEach((el) => {
      const allowed = ALLOWED[el.tagName];
      if (!allowed) {
        if (["SCRIPT", "STYLE", "IFRAME", "OBJECT", "EMBED", "FORM", "INPUT", "BUTTON"].includes(el.tagName)) {
          el.remove();
        } else {
          walk(el);
          el.replaceWith(...Array.from(el.childNodes));
        }
        return;
      }
      Array.from(el.attributes).forEach((attr) => {
        if (!allowed.includes(attr.name)) el.removeAttribute(attr.name);
      });
      if (el.tagName === "A") {
        const href = el.getAttribute("href") ?? "";
        if (!safeUrl(href, false)) el.removeAttribute("href");
        el.setAttribute("target", "_blank");
        el.setAttribute("rel", "noopener noreferrer nofollow");
      }
      if (el.tagName === "IMG") {
        const src = el.getAttribute("src") ?? "";
        if (!safeUrl(src, true)) el.remove();
        else el.setAttribute("loading", "lazy");
      }
      walk(el);
    });
  };
  walk(root);
  return root.innerHTML;
}

/** Đổi văn bản thuần (bài cũ) thành HTML để mở trong trình soạn thảo */
export function textToHtml(text: string): string {
  const esc = (s: string) => s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
  return text
    .split(/\n{2,}/)
    .map((para) => `<p>${esc(para).replace(/\n/g, "<br>")}</p>`)
    .join("");
}

=== FILE: src\utils\imageCompress.ts ===
/**
 * Tự nén ảnh trước khi tải lên (chạy ngay trong trình duyệt, không cần máy chủ).
 *
 * - Ảnh nhỏ gọn sẵn (≤ ~1 MB và không quá lớn về kích thước) được GIỮ NGUYÊN, không nén lại.
 * - Ảnh lớn: thu nhỏ về tối đa `maxDimension` px ở cạnh dài và giảm chất lượng dần
 *   cho đến khi dung lượng ≤ `maxBytes` (mặc định ~1 MB). Tỉ lệ ảnh luôn được giữ nguyên.
 * - Ảnh có nền trong suốt (PNG/WebP) được giữ độ trong suốt; ảnh thường chuyển sang JPEG.
 * - Ảnh GIF / SVG hoặc ảnh không đọc được: giữ nguyên.
 */
export interface CompressOptions {
  /** Dung lượng tối đa mong muốn (byte). Mặc định ~1 MB. */
  maxBytes?: number;
  /** Cạnh dài tối đa (px). Mặc định 1800. */
  maxDimension?: number;
}

export interface CompressResult {
  file: File;
  originalSize: number;
  /** true nếu ảnh đã được nén / thu nhỏ */
  compressed: boolean;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export const IMAGE_MAX_BYTES = 1_000_000;
export const IMAGE_MAX_DIMENSION = 1800;
/** Dung lượng ảnh gốc tối đa cho phép chọn (trước khi nén) */
export const IMAGE_MAX_INPUT_BYTES = 40 * 1024 * 1024;

interface Decoded {
  source: CanvasImageSource;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  close: () => void;
}

async function decode(file: File): Promise<Decoded> {
  if (typeof createImageBitmap === "function") {
    try {
      const bmp = await createImageBitmap(file);
      return { source: bmp, width: bmp.width, height: bmp.height, close: () => bmp.close() };
    } catch {
      /* thử cách dự phòng bên dưới */
    }
  }
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
  try {
    const img = new Image();
    img.decoding = "async";
    img.src = url;
    await img.decode();
    return { source: img, width: img.naturalWidth, height: img.naturalHeight, close: () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url) };
  } catch (e) {
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    throw e;
  }
}

/** Kiểm tra ảnh có điểm trong suốt không (lấy mẫu trên bản thu nhỏ 48px) */
function hasTransparency(source: CanvasImageSource, w: number, h: number): boolean {
  try {
    const s = Math.min(1, 48 / Math.max(w, h));
    const c = document.createElement("canvas");
    c.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * s));
    c.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * s));
    const ctx = c.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
    if (!ctx) return false;
    ctx.drawImage(source, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    const d = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data;
    for (let i = 3; i < d.length; i += 4) if (d[i] < 250) return true;
  } catch {
    /* bỏ qua */
  }
  return false;
}

function toBlob(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, type: string, quality?: number): Promise<Blob | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve, type, quality));
}

const QUALITIES = [0.9, 0.82, 0.74, 0.66, 0.58, 0.5];

export async function compressImage(file: File, opts: CompressOptions = {}): Promise<CompressResult> {
  const maxBytes = opts.maxBytes ?? IMAGE_MAX_BYTES;
  const maxDim = opts.maxDimension ?? IMAGE_MAX_DIMENSION;
  const same: CompressResult = { file, originalSize: file.size, compressed: false, width: 0, height: 0 };

  if (!file.type.startsWith("image/") || file.type === "image/gif" || file.type === "image/svg+xml") return same;

  let dec: Decoded;
  try {
    dec = await decode(file);
  } catch {
    return same; // định dạng trình duyệt không đọc được (ví dụ HEIC) — giữ nguyên
  }

  try {
    const { width: w0, height: h0 } = dec;
    if (!w0 || !h0) return same;

    let scale = Math.min(1, maxDim / Math.max(w0, h0));
    if (scale === 1 && file.size <= maxBytes) return { ...same, width: w0, height: h0 };

    const alpha = /png|webp/.test(file.type) && hasTransparency(dec.source, w0, h0);
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return same;
    ctx.imageSmoothingQuality = "high";

    let outType = alpha ? "image/webp" : "image/jpeg";
    let best: Blob | null = null;
    let bw = w0;
    let bh = h0;

    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 8; attempt++) {
      const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(w0 * scale));
      const h = Math.max(1, Math.round(h0 * scale));
      canvas.width = w;
      canvas.height = h;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      ctx.drawImage(dec.source, 0, 0, w, h);

      for (const q of QUALITIES) {
        let blob = await toBlob(canvas, outType, q);
        if (!blob) continue;
        // Trình duyệt cũ (Safari) không nén được WebP → dùng PNG (chỉ thu nhỏ kích thước để giảm dung lượng)
        if (outType === "image/webp" && blob.type !== "image/webp") {
          outType = "image/png";
          blob = await toBlob(canvas, "image/png");
          if (!blob) break;
        }
        best = blob;
        bw = w;
        bh = h;
        if (blob.size <= maxBytes || outType === "image/png") break;
      }
      if (best && best.size <= maxBytes) break;
      scale *= 0.82;
    }

    if (!best) return same;
    // Ảnh gốc đã nhỏ gọn hơn bản nén → giữ nguyên
    if (file.size <= maxBytes && best.size >= file.size) return { ...same, width: w0, height: h0 };

    const ext = best.type === "image/webp" ? "webp" : best.type === "image/png" ? "png" : "jpg";
    const base = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "") || "image";
    const out = new File([best], `${base}.${ext}`, { type: best.type, lastModified: Date.now() });
    return { file: out, originalSize: file.size, compressed: true, width: bw, height: bh };
  } finally {
    dec.close();
  }
}

export function formatFileSize(n: number): string {
  if (n >= 1024 * 1024) return `${(n / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`;
  if (n >= 1024) return `${Math.round(n / 1024)} KB`;
  return `${n} B`;
}

=== FILE: src\utils\slug.ts ===
/** Bỏ dấu tiếng Việt: "Tâm An Lạc" -> "Tam An Lac" */
export function removeVietnameseTones(input: string): string {
  return input.normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").replace(/đ/g, "d").replace(/Đ/g, "D");
}

export function slugify(input: string): string {
  const base = removeVietnameseTones(input)
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 80);
  const suffix = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  return base ? `${base}-${suffix}` : suffix;
}

/** Tên file an toàn cho Supabase Storage */
export function safeFileName(name: string): string {
  const dot = name.lastIndexOf(".");
  const ext = dot >= 0 ? name.slice(dot).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9.]/g, "") : "";
  const base = removeVietnameseTones(dot >= 0 ? name.slice(0, dot) : name)
    .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 60);
  return `${base || "file"}${ext}`;
}

export function matchSearch(haystack: string, query: string): boolean {
  const k = removeVietnameseTones(query).toLowerCase().trim();
  return !k || removeVietnameseTones(haystack).toLowerCase().includes(k);
}

=== FILE: src\utils\storagePaths.ts ===
/** Mã hoá từng đoạn của đường dẫn (giữ dấu "/") */
export function encodePathClient(path: string): string {
  return path.split("/").map(encodeURIComponent).join("/");
}

const MEDIA = (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MEDIA_URL ?? "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
const LEGACY_MARKER = "/storage/v1/object/public/images/";

/** Lấy đường dẫn trong kho "images" từ URL công khai (Cloudflare R2 hoặc Supabase cũ).
 *  Trả về null nếu ảnh không thuộc kho của mình (ví dụ ảnh Google, link ngoài). */
export function imagePathFromUrl(url: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!url) return null;
  let path: string | null = null;

  if (MEDIA && url.startsWith(`${MEDIA}/images/`)) {
    path = url.slice(`${MEDIA}/images/`.length);
  } else {
    const i = url.indexOf(LEGACY_MARKER);
    if (i >= 0) path = url.slice(i + LEGACY_MARKER.length);
  }
  if (!path) return null;
  path = path.split(/[?#]/)[0];
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(path);
  } catch {
    return path;
  }
}

/** Tất cả ảnh (thuộc kho images) đang được dùng trong một nội dung HTML */
export function imagePathsInHtml(html: string | null | undefined): string[] {
  if (!html) return [];
  const out = new Set<string>();
  for (const m of html.matchAll(/<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']/gi)) {
    const p = imagePathFromUrl(m[1].replace(/&amp;/g, "&"));
    if (p) out.add(p);
  }
  return [...out];
}

/** Phần tử có trong a nhưng không có trong b */
export function difference(a: string[], b: string[]): string[] {
  const set = new Set(b);
  return a.filter((x) => !set.has(x));
}

=== FILE: src\utils\tags.ts ===
export const MAX_TAGS = 8;
export const MAX_TAG_LENGTH = 30;

/** Gợi ý nhanh (thay cho các chuyên mục cũ) */
export const SUGGESTED_TAGS = ["văn", "thơ", "kinh-phật", "thiền", "lời-phật-dạy", "suy-niệm"];

/** "#Kinh Phật " -> "kinh-phật" */
export function normalizeTag(raw: string): string {
  return raw
    .trim()
    .replace(/^#+/, "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, "-")
    .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}_-]/gu, "")
    .replace(/-+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-|-$/g, "")
    .slice(0, MAX_TAG_LENGTH);
}

/** Tách chuỗi nhập (phân cách bằng dấu phẩy, xuống dòng hoặc #) thành danh sách hashtag hợp lệ */
export function parseTags(input: string): string[] {
  return uniqueTags(input.split(/[,\n;#]+/).map(normalizeTag));
}

export function uniqueTags(tags: string[]): string[] {
  return [...new Set(tags.filter(Boolean))].slice(0, MAX_TAGS);
}

-- PHẬT TẠI TÂM v0.4 (đã chạy, KHÔNG cần chạy lại)

create or replace function public.touch_post_updated_at()
returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin
  if (new.title, new.category, new.excerpt, new.content, new.file_path)
     is distinct from (old.title, old.category, old.excerpt, old.content, old.file_path) then
    new.updated_at := now();
  else
    new.updated_at := old.updated_at;
  end if;
  return new;
end; $$;

create or replace function public.touch_song_updated_at()
returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin
  if (new.title, new.artist, new.album, new.audio_path, new.cover_path)
     is distinct from (old.title, old.artist, old.album, old.audio_path, old.cover_path) then
    new.updated_at := now();
  else
    new.updated_at := old.updated_at;
  end if;
  return new;
end; $$;

drop trigger if exists posts_touch_updated_at on public.posts;
create trigger posts_touch_updated_at before update on public.posts
  for each row execute procedure public.touch_post_updated_at();
drop trigger if exists songs_touch_updated_at on public.songs;
create trigger songs_touch_updated_at before update on public.songs
  for each row execute procedure public.touch_song_updated_at();

create or replace function public.touch_song_updated_at()
returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin
  if (new.title, new.artist, new.album_id, new.lyrics, new.track_no, new.audio_path, new.cover_path)
     is distinct from (old.title, old.artist, old.album_id, old.lyrics, old.track_no, old.audio_path, old.cover_path) then

  else
    new.updated_at := old.updated_at;
  end if;
  return new;
end; $$;

-- 5) BÀI VIẾT: lượt đọc, ảnh đại diện ----------------------------------
alter table public.posts add column if not exists view_count int not null default 0;
alter table public.posts add column if not exists cover_image text;

-- 6) ĐẾM LƯỢT ĐỌC / NGHE (ai cũng gọi được, chỉ tăng 1) ----------------
create or replace function public.increment_post_view(p_id uuid)
returns void language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  update public.posts set view_count = view_count + 1 where id = p_id and is_published;
$$;
create or replace function public.increment_song_play(s_id uuid)
returns void language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  update public.songs set play_count = play_count + 1 where id = s_id and is_published;
$$;
grant execute on function public.increment_post_view(uuid) to anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.increment_song_play(uuid) to anon, authenticated;

-- 7) YÊU THÍCH ---------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.likes (
  user_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  target_type text not null check (target_type in ('post','song','album')),
  target_id uuid not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (user_id, target_type, target_id)
);
create index if not exists likes_target_idx on public.likes (target_type, target_id);
alter table public.likes enable row level security;
drop policy if exists "read likes" on public.likes;
create policy "read likes" on public.likes for select using (true);
drop policy if exists "like" on public.likes;
create policy "like" on public.likes for insert to authenticated with check (user_id = auth.uid());
drop policy if exists "unlike" on public.likes;
create policy "unlike" on public.likes for delete to authenticated using (user_id = auth.uid());

-- 8) BÌNH LUẬN ---------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.comments (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  target_type text not null check (target_type in ('post','song','album')),
  target_id uuid not null,
  body text not null check (char_length(body) between 1 and 2000),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index if not exists comments_target_idx on public.comments (target_type, target_id, created_at);
alter table public.comments enable row level security;
drop policy if exists "read comments" on public.comments;
create policy "read comments" on public.comments for select using (true);
drop policy if exists "write comment" on public.comments;
create policy "write comment" on public.comments for insert to authenticated with check (user_id = auth.uid());
drop policy if exists "delete comment" on public.comments;
create policy "delete comment" on public.comments for delete to authenticated
  using (user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());

-- 9) PLAYLIST ----------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.playlists (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  title text not null,
  is_public boolean not null default true,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create table if not exists public.playlist_songs (
  playlist_id uuid not null references public.playlists(id) on delete cascade,
  song_id uuid not null references public.songs(id) on delete cascade,
  added_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (playlist_id, song_id)
);
alter table public.playlists enable row level security;
alter table public.playlist_songs enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "read playlists" on public.playlists;
create policy "read playlists" on public.playlists for select using (is_public or owner_id = auth.uid());
drop policy if exists "insert playlists" on public.playlists;
create policy "insert playlists" on public.playlists for insert to authenticated with check (owner_id = auth.uid());
drop policy if exists "update playlists" on public.playlists;
create policy "update playlists" on public.playlists for update to authenticated using (owner_id = auth.uid());
drop policy if exists "delete playlists" on public.playlists;
create policy "delete playlists" on public.playlists for delete to authenticated using (owner_id = auth.uid());

drop policy if exists "read playlist songs" on public.playlist_songs;
create policy "read playlist songs" on public.playlist_songs for select using (
  exists (select 1 from public.playlists p where p.id = playlist_id and (p.is_public or p.owner_id = auth.uid())));
drop policy if exists "add playlist songs" on public.playlist_songs;
create policy "add playlist songs" on public.playlist_songs for insert to authenticated with check (
  exists (select 1 from public.playlists p where p.id = playlist_id and p.owner_id = auth.uid()));
drop policy if exists "remove playlist songs" on public.playlist_songs;
create policy "remove playlist songs" on public.playlist_songs for delete to authenticated using (
  exists (select 1 from public.playlists p where p.id = playlist_id and p.owner_id = auth.uid()));

-- =====================================================================
-- PHẬT TẠI TÂM v0.6 — Hashtag + Tác giả, Điều lệ, Radio do quản trị chọn
-- Chạy 1 lần trong Supabase → SQL Editor (SAU v0.5). An toàn nếu chạy lại.
-- =====================================================================

-- 1) BÀI VIẾT: bỏ chuyên mục cứng -> hashtag; thêm "Tác giả" ---------------
alter table public.posts add column if not exists tags text[] not null default '{}';
alter table public.posts add column if not exists author_name text;
alter table public.posts alter column category drop not null;
alter table public.posts drop constraint if exists posts_category_check;

-- Chuyển chuyên mục cũ thành hashtag
update public.posts
set tags = array[
  case category
    when 'van'       then 'văn'
    when 'tho'       then 'thơ'
    when 'kinh-phat' then 'kinh-phật'
    when 'thien'     then 'thiền'
  end]
where cardinality(tags) = 0 and category in ('van','tho','kinh-phat','thien');

-- "Tác giả" mặc định = tên người đăng (có thể sửa sau)
update public.posts p
set author_name = pr.full_name
from public.profiles pr
where p.author_id = pr.id and p.author_name is null;

create index if not exists posts_tags_idx on public.posts using gin (tags);

-- Ngày "sửa lần cuối" cũng thay đổi khi sửa hashtag / tác giả
create or replace function public.touch_post_updated_at()
returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin
  if (new.title, new.tags, new.author_name, new.excerpt, new.content, new.file_path)
     is distinct from (old.title, old.tags, old.author_name, old.excerpt, old.content, old.file_path) then
    new.updated_at := now();
  else
    new.updated_at := old.updated_at;
  end if;
  return new;
end; $$;

-- 2) ĐIỀU LỆ: lưu việc đồng ý -----------------------------------------------
alter table public.profiles add column if not exists terms_accepted_at timestamptz;
alter table public.profiles add column if not exists terms_version text;

-- Người dùng KHÔNG tự ghi được cột này; chỉ qua hàm dưới (ghi giờ máy chủ)
create or replace function public.accept_terms(p_version text)
returns void language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  update public.profiles
  set terms_accepted_at = now(), terms_version = p_version
  where id = auth.uid();
$$;
revoke all on function public.accept_terms(text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.accept_terms(text) to authenticated;

-- Đăng ký bằng email có ô tích đồng ý -> ghi nhận ngay khi tạo hồ sơ
create or replace function public.handle_new_user()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
begin
  insert into public.profiles (id, full_name, avatar_url, terms_accepted_at, terms_version)
  values (
    new.id,
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', new.raw_user_meta_data->>'name', split_part(new.email,'@',1)),
    new.raw_user_meta_data->>'avatar_url',
    case when new.raw_user_meta_data->>'terms_version' is not null then now() end,
    new.raw_user_meta_data->>'terms_version'
  )
  on conflict (id) do nothing;
  return new;
end; $$;

-- 3) RADIO do quản trị viên chọn ----------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.radio_settings (
  id int primary key default 1 check (id = 1),
  mode text not null default 'all' check (mode in ('all','custom')),
  shuffle boolean not null default true,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
insert into public.radio_settings (id) values (1) on conflict (id) do nothing;

create table if not exists public.radio_songs (
  song_id uuid primary key references public.songs(id) on delete cascade,
  position int not null default 0,
  added_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.radio_settings enable row level security;
alter table public.radio_songs enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "read radio settings" on public.radio_settings;
create policy "read radio settings" on public.radio_settings for select using (true);
drop policy if exists "admin write radio settings" on public.radio_settings;
create policy "admin write radio settings" on public.radio_settings for all to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin()) with check (public.is_admin());

drop policy if exists "read radio songs" on public.radio_songs;
create policy "read radio songs" on public.radio_songs for select using (true);
drop policy if exists "admin write radio songs" on public.radio_songs;
create policy "admin write radio songs" on public.radio_songs for all to authenticated
  using (public.is_admin()) with check (public.is_admin());

=== FILE: supabase\migration_v8_solo.sql ===
-- =====================================================================
-- PHẬT TẠI TÂM v0.9 — Mỗi bài hát không có album sẽ tự thành "Single" (album solo)
-- Chạy 1 lần trong Supabase → SQL Editor. An toàn nếu chạy lại.
-- =====================================================================

-- 1) Đánh dấu album solo ---------------------------------------------
alter table public.albums add column if not exists is_solo boolean not null default false;
create index if not exists albums_solo_idx on public.albums (is_solo);

-- Album solo chỉ hiện công khai khi bài hát của nó đã được duyệt
-- (tránh lộ tên bài hát đang chờ duyệt vì album solo lấy tên theo bài hát)
drop policy if exists "read albums" on public.albums;
create policy "read albums" on public.albums for select using (
  not is_solo
  or owner_id = auth.uid()
  or public.is_admin()
  or exists (select 1 from public.songs s where s.album_id = albums.id and s.is_published)
);

-- 2) Tự tạo album solo khi bài hát chưa thuộc album nào ----------------
create or replace function public.songs_ensure_album()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare new_album uuid;
begin
  -- (bỏ qua nếu chủ bài hát đã bị xoá — đang xoá tài khoản kéo theo xoá bài)
  if new.album_id is null and exists (select 1 from public.profiles p where p.id = new.uploader_id) then
    insert into public.albums (owner_id, title, is_solo, created_at, updated_at)
    values (new.uploader_id, new.title, true, coalesce(new.created_at, now()), now())
    returning id into new_album;
    new.album_id := new_album;
  end if;
  return new;
end; $$;

drop trigger if exists songs_ensure_album on public.songs;
create trigger songs_ensure_album
  before insert or update of album_id on public.songs
  for each row when (new.album_id is null)
  execute function public.songs_ensure_album();

-- 3) Đổi tên bài hát -> đổi tên album solo; chuyển bài sang album khác -> xoá album solo cũ (rỗng)
create or replace function public.songs_sync_solo()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
begin
  if new.album_id is not null and new.title is distinct from old.title then
    update public.albums set title = new.title where id = new.album_id and is_solo;
  end if;
  if old.album_id is not null and old.album_id is distinct from new.album_id then
    delete from public.albums a
    where a.id = old.album_id and a.is_solo
      and not exists (select 1 from public.songs s where s.album_id = a.id);
  end if;
  return new;
end; $$;

drop trigger if exists songs_sync_solo on public.songs;
create trigger songs_sync_solo
  after update of title, album_id on public.songs
  for each row execute function public.songs_sync_solo();

-- 4) Xoá bài hát -> xoá album solo của nó
create or replace function public.songs_cleanup_solo()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
begin
  if old.album_id is not null then
    delete from public.albums a
    where a.id = old.album_id and a.is_solo
      and not exists (select 1 from public.songs s where s.album_id = a.id);
  end if;
  return old;
end; $$;

drop trigger if exists songs_cleanup_solo on public.songs;
create trigger songs_cleanup_solo
  after delete on public.songs
  for each row execute function public.songs_cleanup_solo();

-- 5) Chuyển các bài hát hiện có (chưa thuộc album nào) thành album solo ---
-- Tạm tắt trigger "ngày sửa" để các bài không bị hiện "Sửa lần cuối" vì thay đổi này.
alter table public.songs disable trigger songs_touch_updated_at;

update public.songs set album_id = null where album_id is null;   -- kích hoạt trigger tạo album solo

alter table public.songs enable trigger songs_touch_updated_at;

-- 6) Kiểm tra: kết quả phải là 0
select count(*) as songs_without_album from public.songs where album_id is null;

-- PHẬT TẠI TÂM — schema gốc (đã chạy ở v0.2, KHÔNG cần chạy lại)
create table if not exists public.profiles (
  id uuid primary key references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  full_name text,
  avatar_url text,
  role text not null default 'user' check (role in ('user','admin')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create or replace function public.is_admin()
returns boolean language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select exists (select 1 from public.profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin');
$$;

create or replace function public.handle_new_user()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
begin
  insert into public.profiles (id, full_name, avatar_url)
  values (new.id,
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', new.raw_user_meta_data->>'name', split_part(new.email,'@',1)),
    new.raw_user_meta_data->>'avatar_url')
  on conflict (id) do nothing;
  return new;
end; $$;

drop trigger if exists on_auth_user_created on auth.users;
create trigger on_auth_user_created after insert on auth.users
  for each row execute procedure public.handle_new_user();

alter table public.profiles enable row level security;
drop policy if exists "profiles readable" on public.profiles;
create policy "profiles readable" on public.profiles for select using (true);
drop policy if exists "update own profile" on public.profiles;
create policy "update own profile" on public.profiles for update to authenticated
  using (id = auth.uid()) with check (id = auth.uid());
revoke update on public.profiles from authenticated;
grant update (full_name, avatar_url) on public.profiles to authenticated;

create table if not exists public.posts (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  author_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  title text not null,
  slug text not null unique,
  category text not null check (category in ('van','tho','kinh-phat','thien')),
  excerpt text,
  content text not null default '',
  file_path text,
  file_name text,
  is_published boolean not null default false,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index if not exists posts_category_idx on public.posts (category, created_at desc);
alter table public.posts enable row level security;
drop policy if exists "read posts" on public.posts;
create policy "read posts" on public.posts for select
  using (is_published or author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());
drop policy if exists "insert posts" on public.posts;
create policy "insert posts" on public.posts for insert to authenticated
  with check (author_id = auth.uid() and (is_published = false or public.is_admin()));
drop policy if exists "update posts" on public.posts;
create policy "update posts" on public.posts for update to authenticated
  using (author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin())
  with check (public.is_admin() or (author_id = auth.uid() and is_published = false));
drop policy if exists "delete posts" on public.posts;
create policy "delete posts" on public.posts for delete to authenticated
  using (author_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());

create table if not exists public.songs (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  uploader_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  title text not null,
  artist text,
  album text,
  audio_path text not null,
  cover_path text,
  is_published boolean not null default false,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table public.songs enable row level security;
drop policy if exists "read songs" on public.songs;
create policy "read songs" on public.songs for select
  using (is_published or uploader_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());
drop policy if exists "insert songs" on public.songs;
create policy "insert songs" on public.songs for insert to authenticated
  with check (uploader_id = auth.uid() and (is_published = false or public.is_admin()));
drop policy if exists "update songs" on public.songs;
create policy "update songs" on public.songs for update to authenticated
  using (uploader_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin())
  with check (public.is_admin() or (uploader_id = auth.uid() and is_published = false));
drop policy if exists "delete songs" on public.songs;
create policy "delete songs" on public.songs for delete to authenticated
  using (uploader_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());

insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public)
values ('books','books',true), ('music','music',true), ('covers','covers',true)
on conflict (id) do nothing;
